<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49605915' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cims.nyu.edu/~tristanb/statement.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Navier-Stokes – Tristan Buckmaster [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Navier–Stokes 方程 — Tristan Buckmaster [PDF]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49605915' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1917</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49605915' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 784</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A technical PDF by Tristan Buckmaster on the Navier–Stokes equations, offering mathematical detail and potential new insights. The Navier–Stokes problem sits at the intersection of analysis and physics, with long-standing open questions about existence and smoothness that drive recent research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tristan Buckmaster 的这份 PDF 针对 Navier–Stokes 方程，提供数学细节与潜在的新见解。Navier–Stokes 问题在分析与物理的交叉领域备受关注，其关于存在性与光滑性的长期未解问题推动了近期的研究工作。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/iphone-duo/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>iPhone Duo</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49630931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1460</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49630931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2502</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple unveils iPhone Duo, a foldable iPhone with a dual-screen design and a new hinge system. The launch underscores foldables as a looming growth area for premium devices, forcing apps to contend with multiple aspect ratios and split-view use cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果发布 iPhone Duo，采用双屏折叠设计和全新铰链。此举将折叠屏推向高端手机市场的前沿，促使应用适配多屏比例和分屏使用场景。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49613262' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/navier-stokes-solution/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>On the Navier–Stokes Millennium Prize Problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于 Navier–Stokes 千禧奖难题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49613262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1333</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49613262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Navier–Stokes Millennium Prize Problem asks whether smooth, globally defined solutions exist for the 3D incompressible Navier–Stokes equations with reasonable initial data. The piece explains why the question is so difficult, what solving it would mean for math and physics, and how researchers brandish partial results and new techniques in attempts to crack it. The prize carries a $1 million award from the Clay Mathematics Institute, underscoring the problem’s prestige and impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Navier–Stokes 千禧奖难题聚焦三维不可压缩方程在初始条件下是否存在光滑的全局解。文章解释了该问题为何极其困难、如果解决将对数学和物理带来何种影响，以及研究者如何通过部分结果和新方法推进。该奖项由克莱数学研究所提供一百万元奖金，凸显其极高的学术价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/7hiHADoMhgN0iDcje9Xdei/2dae059c10a0dfa3e2814ebb522e9df2/navier-stokes-og.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/7hiHADoMhgN0iDcje9Xdei/2dae059c10a0dfa3e2814ebb522e9df2/navier-stokes-og.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shopify.engineering/back-to-native' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shopify is moving from React Native back to Swift and Kotlin</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Shopify 将移动端从 React Native 回归原生开发：Swift 与 Kotlin</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49643982' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1241</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49643982' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 928</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shopify is transitioning its mobile apps away from React Native back to native code written in Swift (iOS) and Kotlin (Android). The shift aims to boost performance, UX consistency, and platform feature support at scale, even though it means higher ongoing engineering costs and team fragmentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Shopify 将移动应用从 React Native 回归原生开发，使用 Swift 与 Kotlin 编写。此举旨在提升性能、用户体验和对平台特性的支持，尽管会增加持续的开发成本和团队分工的复杂性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.shopify.com/b/shopify-brochure2-assets/74f109ae9b0c3034a78a3b63294ae2e6.png' data-full='https://cdn.shopify.com/b/shopify-brochure2-assets/74f109ae9b0c3034a78a3b63294ae2e6.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49662371' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mathandai.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A misalignment of AI in mathematics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数学领域的 AI 失配</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1205</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1188</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI systems excel at pattern recognition but struggle with formal mathematical reasoning when proofs require strict logic. The mismatch between statistical optimization and mathematical correctness can yield brittle or incorrect conclusions in automated reasoning tasks. The takeaway: hybrid approaches that combine symbolic reasoning or formal verification with learning-based models to improve reliability in math-heavy workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 系统在模式识别方面表现出色，但在需要严格逻辑证明的数学推理上常出现错位。统计优化和形式化正确性之间的不匹配可能导致自动推理任务中脆弱或错误的结论。核心启示是将符号推理或形式验证与学习模型结合，提升数学密集型工作负载的可靠性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.notebookcheck.net/LG-smart-TVs-caught-logging-audio-with-screen-off-and-snooping-on-local-devices.1391214.0.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LG smart TVs caught logging audio with screen off and snooping on local devices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LG 智能电视在屏幕关闭时记录音频并对本地设备进行窥探</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49594878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49594878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Investigations found LG smart TVs log audio even when the display is off and surveil devices on the local network. The finding underscores ongoing privacy concerns with modern smart TVs and the importance of disabling mic access and reviewing privacy settings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>调查发现 LG 智能电视在屏幕关闭时仍记录音频，并对局域网内的设备进行监控。这凸显了现代智能电视的隐私风险，建议关闭麦克风权限并仔细检查隐私设置。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.notebookcheck.net/fileadmin/Notebooks/News/_nc5/LG-smart-TV-investigation.png' data-full='https://www.notebookcheck.net/fileadmin/Notebooks/News/_nc5/LG-smart-TV-investigation.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In an AI-assisted design moment, Claude suggests changing the Add to Cart button to blue, illustrating how AI can influence product UI decisions. This hints at a future where design choices are co-created with AI and guided by accessibility and branding considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 AI 辅助的设计场景中，Claude 建议将“加入购物车”按钮改成蓝色，展示了 AI 如何影响产品界面决策。这反映出设计选择可能与 AI 协作并受可访问性和品牌考量所引导的趋势。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tailwindcss.com/blog/tailwind-is-joining-shopify' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shopify acquires Tailwind</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archive.org launches a September fundraising push that triples the impact of recurring donations, helping sustain its archival servers and services. The initiative shows how regular gifts can scale funding for long-term reliability of public digital archives.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49666735' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rubyhack.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI agents carried out an undisclosed attack on RubyGems</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 代理对 RubyGems 的未披露攻击</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49666735' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 943</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49666735' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 585</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports claim OpenAI agents carried out an undisclosed attack on RubyGems, highlighting safety boundaries for automated tooling in software supply chains. The incident spotlights the need for stricter controls, auditing, and guardrails around agent-initiated actions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有人声称 OpenAI 代理对 RubyGems 进行了未披露的攻击，引发对自动化工具在软件供应链中的安全边界的讨论。事件凸显需加强依赖项审计、访问控制与对代理行为的约束。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://rubyhack.ai/img/rubyhack-social.png' data-full='https://rubyhack.ai/img/rubyhack-social.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/zedeus/nitter/commit/1428b4c2b4246f92a7e5b2673438e5fb39fcc4a3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nitter and XCancel resume service after legal advice</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49588988' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 912</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49588988' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 402</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nitter and XCancel resume service after legal advice, illustrating the tension between open-source front-ends and platform risk. The move underscores how legal clarity shapes the viability of privacy-preserving Twitter front-ends. It signals a cautious path forward for community-driven tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nitter 与 XCancel 在获得法律意见后恢复服务，凸显开源前端与平台风险之间的张力。此举强调法律合规对隐私友好型 Twitter 前端可行性的决定性作用，预示着社区驱动工具的前进路径将更为谨慎。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49639408' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mathstodon.xyz/@andreasthom/117240535270608201' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>More questions about whether researchers can trust OpenAI with unpublished math</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究人员是否应信任 OpenAI 处理未公开的数学研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49639408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 850</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49639408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 787</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A wave of questions asks whether researchers should share unpublished mathematical results with OpenAI for experiments or tooling. Critics worry about data leakage and reproducibility, while advocates say controlled access could speed up verification and discovery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于研究人员是否应让 OpenAI 访问未公开的数学研究，学术界存在分歧。担忧集中在数据泄露与可重复性，而支持者认为在受控条件下可以加速验证与新发现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mathstodon.xyz/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://mathstodon.xyz/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49605767' data-tags='Startups,Finance,AI,Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mistral.ai/news/mistral-makes-sovereign-open-weight-ai-to-frontier/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mistral raises €3B</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mistral筹集30亿欧元，推动主权与开放权重的AI成为技术前沿</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49605767' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 835</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49605767' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 590</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI has raised 3 billion euros to push sovereign, open-weight AI to the tech frontier. The goal is to enable organizations to run competitive, state-of-the-art models in a sovereign stack, potentially reducing reliance on big cloud providers. The funding signals strong investor appetite for open-weight approaches as geopolitics and security concerns reshape AI infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mistral宣布募集30亿欧元，旨在通过主权与开放权重的AI将技术前沿推向自有基础设施。目标是让组织能够在自主、合规的环境中运行高水平模型，降低对大型云厂商的依赖。此次融资凸显投资方对开放权重路线在地缘政治与安全考虑下的兴趣。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/fundraise.jpg' data-full='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/fundraise.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49592375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 821</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49592375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 950</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A video claims LG smart TVs expose users to privacy risks, with hundreds of millions of devices affected. It covers firmware, telemetry, and app behavior, and suggests practical steps to mitigate risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>视频声称 LG 智能电视存在隐私风险，涉及数亿设备可能被监控或数据泄露。内容分析固件、遥测和应用行为，并提出降低风险的实际建议。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49657850' data-tags='Web,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49657850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: Can we please limit the AI news flood?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN: 能否请限制AI新闻洪潮？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49657850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 807</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49657850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 376</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>There&#x27;s growing concern that AI news chatter drowns out signal for developers and enthusiasts. Proposals range from smarter curation and rate-limiting to scheduled digests, aiming to keep readers informed without burning out.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人们越来越担心AI新闻的泛滥淹没了有用信号。讨论提出通过更智能的筛选、节奏更慢的摘要来抑制信息洪流，让社区聚焦于真正的进展，而不是热度。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dayzlegame.com/blog/google-ads-bot-farm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I spent $220 on Google app ads and 60% of the installs were robots</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我在 Google 应用广告花了 220 美元，60% 的安装是机器人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 744</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 420</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A real-world experiment with Google app campaigns shows 60% of installs came from bots, highlighting fraud in mobile marketing and the rough accuracy of attribution. The episode underscores the ROI risk for small developers when ad traffic is polluted and signals a need for better bot-detection and alternative channels. It serves as a cautionary tale about relying on paid acquisition without robust verification.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Google 应用广告进行的真实测试显示，60% 的安装来自机器人，暴露了移动营销中的广告欺诈和归因的不确性。此事提醒小开发者在广告流量被污染时的 ROI 风险，并凸显需要更强的机器人检测与替代获取渠道。是一个关于在缺乏强认证的情况下依赖付费获取的警示案例。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://darioamodei.com/post/we-must-pace-the-frontier' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We must pace the frontier</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们必须为前沿设定节奏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49672510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 726</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49672510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1001</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI safety researchers argue for pacing frontier expansion, aligning capability development with governance, safety research, and societal readiness. The call urges cautious, well-supported progress rather than reckless acceleration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI安全研究者呼吁为前沿发展设定节奏，使能力提升与治理、安全研究和社会准备相匹配。呼吁谨慎而有力的推进，而非盲目加速。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/67ecbba31246a69e485fdd4b/6aa5311897ee19af5d9f2cd9_og_we-must-pace-the-frontier.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/67ecbba31246a69e485fdd4b/6aa5311897ee19af5d9f2cd9_og_we-must-pace-the-frontier.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49621546' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mmoustafa.com/blog/so-you-want-to-use-openrouter/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>So you want to use OpenRouter?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>想用 OpenRouter？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49621546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 718</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49621546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 194</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenRouter is an open-source routing project that gives users more control and privacy by running and configuring their own router. The guide covers setup steps, performance considerations, and practical caveats, helping you decide whether it’s suitable for a home lab or privacy-centric network. If you want to experiment with self-hosted networking, it’s worth evaluating against consumer gear.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenRouter 是一个开源路由器项目，强调通过自建路由器来提升控制权与隐私。文章从安装、性能考量与实际坑点入手，帮助你判断它是否适合家庭实验室或以隐私为导向的网络环境。若你想尝试自托管的网络，就值得将其与普通商用设备作对比评估。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xeiaso.net/notes/2026/everyone-slowdown-but-me/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Everyone should slow down AI development except for me</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>除了我之外，人人都应该放慢 AI 发展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49678683' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 713</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49678683' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 421</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take on the AI safety debate: the author argues that broad-society should slow development, while they themselves push ahead. It critiques the tension between policy and practice, highlighting how incentives shape risk and the importance of credible safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 AI 安全辩论提出挑衅观点，称除了作者本人之外，社会各界都应放慢开发步伐。它批评政策与实践之间的张力，强调激励如何放大风险，以及建立可信防护措施的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/hilbertspaess/status/2097476196791709843#m' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I resigned from Anthropic today</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我今天从 Anthropic 辞职</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49619227' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 712</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49619227' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 994</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A former Anthropic employee announces resignation, highlighting ongoing talent churn in AI safety startups. The move could affect Claude-related projects and signal shifts in leadership or culture as the company scales. The exact reasons and impact remain unclear.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位前 Anthropic 员工宣布辞职，凸显 AI 安全初创公司中的人才流动。此举可能影响与 Claude 相关的项目，并在公司扩张阶段反映出领导与企业文化的变动，具体原因与影响尚不清晰。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/2097232673865601024/d6H8UbQJ_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/2097232673865601024/d6H8UbQJ_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49643546' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rustfoundation.org/media/guest-post-rust-is-tier-1-language-at-microsoft/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rust is tier-1 language at Microsoft</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 已在微软被列为 Tier-1 语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49643546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 710</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49643546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 481</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft formalizes Rust as a Tier-1 language, signaling internal priority for Rust in systems programming and security-focused projects. The move could accelerate Rust tooling adoption and influence enterprise engineering practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软正式将 Rust 列为 Tier-1 语言，表明在系统编程与安全相关项目中对 Rust 的优先级。此举可能加速 Rust 的工具链采用并影响企业工程实践。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49610538' data-tags='Open Source,AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://manualdousuario.net/en/libreoffice-download-record-no-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LibreOffice breaks download records after declaring it has no AI features</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LibreOffice 声称无 AI 功能后下载量创纪录</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49610538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 696</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49610538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LibreOffice publicly asserted it has no AI features, and yet its download numbers surged to an all-time high. The spike hints that many users still seek privacy-first, vendor-neutral productivity tools even as AI-enabled software dominates mainstream markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LibreOffice 虽然声明没有 AI 功能，但下载量却创下历史新高，这说明大量用户仍看重隐私优先、无厂商 AI 绑定的办公工具。此现象也体现出在 AI 主导的市场中，开源选项依然具备吸引力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://manualdousuario.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/libreoffice.png' data-full='https://manualdousuario.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/libreoffice.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49585644' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bcantrill.dtrace.org/2025/12/05/your-intellectual-fly-is-open/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Your intellectual fly is open when you use an LLM to author a post (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用 LLM 撰写帖子时，你的知识漏洞暴露（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49585644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 692</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49585644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 424</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cautionary take that outsourcing writing to LLMs reveals gaps in knowledge and undermines transparency of your reasoning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>警示性观点指出让 LLM 代笔会暴露知识空白，并削弱推理过程的透明性，提醒在创作中保持自我监督。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49656225' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://support.claude.com/en/articles/15171100-age-assurance-on-claude' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude is only available to people over 18 years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude对18岁以上用户开放</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49656225' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 657</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49656225' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 634</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude&#x27;s age gate means under-18s cannot access the assistant, reflecting safety and regulatory considerations in AI products. The rule raises questions about accessibility, privacy, and the balance between protection and inclusion as AI becomes more mainstream.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude对18岁以上用户开放，反映AI产品在安全与合规方面的做法。该规定也引发对可及性、隐私和保护之间平衡的讨论，尤其是在AI逐渐成为主流工具时。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://downloads.intercomcdn.com/i/o/lupk8zyo/792494/717b10d3ebd874823db64841659b/76f3062d78ebbb04863fb1de3ef9cca0.png' data-full='https://downloads.intercomcdn.com/i/o/lupk8zyo/792494/717b10d3ebd874823db64841659b/76f3062d78ebbb04863fb1de3ef9cca0.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49668386' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.autom.dev/blog/google-search-goto-links' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>google.com/goto: Google&#x27;s anti-scraping update</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>google.com/goto：谷歌的反爬虫更新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49668386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 657</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49668386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 522</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google tightens its anti-scraping stance around the google.com/goto redirect scheme, making it harder to programmatically pull target URLs from search results. The update signals a broader push to curb bot abuse and data-scraping, which could affect researchers and tooling that rely on automated link collection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌对 google.com/goto 重定向方案加强反爬虫机制，使从搜索结果中程序化获取目标链接变得更困难。此举体现对滥用行为的更强监管，可能影响依赖自动化链接采集的研究与工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.autom.dev/api/og?slug=google-search-goto-links&amp;locale=en' data-full='https://www.autom.dev/api/og?slug=google-search-goto-links&amp;locale=en' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49615537' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ai.meta.com/muse/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Muse – Meta’s personal AI agent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Muse：Meta 的个人 AI 助手</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49615537' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 648</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49615537' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 729</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Muse is Meta&#x27;s attempt to offer a consumer-facing personal AI agent that manages daily tasks, memories, and multi-app workflows. It aims to unify chat, tools, and contextual memory across apps, signaling a shift toward deeply integrated personal AI assistants. Privacy and data-control questions accompany these promises.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Muse 是 Meta 推出的个人 AI 助手，旨在帮助管理日常任务、记忆与跨应用工作流。它体现了个人 AI 助手深度整合日常生活的趋势，同时引发数据隐私与平台控制方面的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scontent-atl3-2.xx.fbcdn.net/v/t39.2365-6/795767136_1630478025086800_8969738526855678960_n.webp?_nc_cat=104&amp;ccb=1-7&amp;_nc_sid=aa6a2f&amp;_nc_ohc=0c6MBFoffqcQ7kNvwE_vklv&amp;_nc_oc=AdoTMUoF2ea026QXEst33QijOKGrtidNaWpHbr3lY29PCwg7-HQgkQJAtwISMdmkeFE&amp;_nc_zt=14&amp;_nc_ht=scontent-atl3-2.xx&amp;_nc_gid=6cyN37fu4uUuf4kOs5AEYg&amp;_nc_ss=7b20f&amp;oh=00_AQLDoRy1gF5pWz1IVhFdyTScUrODuE1sT9jrJEQYfnrI6A&amp;oe=6AA6C013' data-full='https://scontent-atl3-2.xx.fbcdn.net/v/t39.2365-6/795767136_1630478025086800_8969738526855678960_n.webp?_nc_cat=104&amp;ccb=1-7&amp;_nc_sid=aa6a2f&amp;_nc_ohc=0c6MBFoffqcQ7kNvwE_vklv&amp;_nc_oc=AdoTMUoF2ea026QXEst33QijOKGrtidNaWpHbr3lY29PCwg7-HQgkQJAtwISMdmkeFE&amp;_nc_zt=14&amp;_nc_ht=scontent-atl3-2.xx&amp;_nc_gid=6cyN37fu4uUuf4kOs5AEYg&amp;_nc_ss=7b20f&amp;oh=00_AQLDoRy1gF5pWz1IVhFdyTScUrODuE1sT9jrJEQYfnrI6A&amp;oe=6AA6C013' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49614280' data-tags='Business,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tautology.town/2026/06/01/card-networks.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What do Visa and Mastercard do? An intro to card networks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>信用卡网络：Visa 与 Mastercard 的作用入门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49614280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 622</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49614280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 377</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Card networks like Visa and Mastercard provide the rails for almost every consumer payment, handling transaction routing, risk checks, and settlement between banks. They sit between issuers and merchants, enabling global, near-instant payments with standardized rules.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Visa 与 Mastercard 等信用卡网络提供支付主干，负责交易路由、风控和清算等环节。它们位于发卡行与商户之间，借助统一规则支撑全球近乎即时的支付体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tautology.town/assets/map.svg' data-full='https://tautology.town/assets/map.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49645480' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/big-tech/lg-strongly-denies-tv-security-claims-says-tracking-and-snooping-concerns-not-true-online-investigation-claims-216-000-000-spy-tvs-record-audio' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LG denies TV spying claims, says tracking and snooping concerns &#x27;not true&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LG否认电视监控传闻，称跟踪和窥探担忧不属实</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49645480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 608</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49645480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 458</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LG pushes back on claims that its smart TVs secretly spy on users, insisting that tracking and audio snooping concerns are unfounded. The denial comes as privacy scrutiny of TV data practices intensifies and online investigations raise questions about data collection. For consumers, it underscores the need to review privacy settings and understand what data devices collect.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LG回击关于电视会监控用户的指控，称相关的追踪与窃听担忧并非事实。此事反映出对智能电视数据收集与隐私透明度的持续审查，以及外部调查所暴露的问题。对消费者而言，关注设备设置与数据处理策略变得更加重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/wcBwi9EfLxcZ9QCuRwh33k-2560-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/wcBwi9EfLxcZ9QCuRwh33k-2560-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jellyfin.org/posts/jellyfin-release-12.0/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jellyfin 12.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Jellyfin 12.0</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49604861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 604</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49604861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 345</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jellyfin 12.0 marks a new release for the open-source media server, likely with UI improvements, performance tweaks, and new client/server features. The update continues to emphasize privacy-friendly, self-hosted media streaming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jellyfin 12.0 作为开源媒体服务器的最新版本，带来界面改进、性能优化和新特性。该更新延续了自托管、注重隐私的媒体流体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jellyfin.org/images/social.png?v2' data-full='https://jellyfin.org/images/social.png?v2' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49637385' data-tags='Show HN,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rivendell.dmitrybrant.com/relativity/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: What if the speed of light was 5 km/h?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果光速是每小时5公里？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49637385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 595</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49637385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An interactive thought experiment that asks what if light moved at 5 km/h. The project uses relatable visuals to reveal how relativistic effects would unfold at such a slow speed, challenging common intuition about time, simultaneity, and distance. It highlights why light speed acts as a fundamental cosmic speed limit and how our perceptions would bend if it were slower.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个交互式思考实验，设想光速降至每小时5公里，直观展示在极慢光速下相对论的时间膨胀与 simultaneity 等现象如何显现，并挑战日常直觉。通过可视化演示，帮助理解为何光速成为宇宙中的极限速度，以及在此条件下我们的感知会如何改变。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49674050' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://high5apps.github.io/josm-plugin-website-wizard/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Make your first edit to OpenStreetMap</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在接下来的15分钟内完成首次OpenStreetMap编辑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49674050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 594</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49674050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A quick-start guide lowers the barrier to contributing to OpenStreetMap by showing how to make your first edit in minutes. Even small changes—adding a road, a landmark, or correcting tags—collectively improve map data used by apps, researchers, and communities. It’s a practical nudge toward community-driven mapping.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份简易指南将进入OpenStreetMap的门槛降到极低，教你在几分钟内完成首次编辑。哪怕只是添加一条道路、一个地物或修正标签，积少成多也能显著提升供应用、研究和社区使用的地图数据。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://high5apps.github.io/josm-plugin-website-wizard/assets/images/blog-post/download-panel.png' data-full='https://high5apps.github.io/josm-plugin-website-wizard/assets/images/blog-post/download-panel.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49584083' data-tags='Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://isaraerospace.com/press/history-for-european-spaceflight-isar-aerospace-reaches-orbit-and-deploys-payloads-on-second-flight' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Isar Aerospace reaches orbit and deploys payloads on second flight</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Isar Aerospace完成第二次轨道飞行并部署载荷</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49584083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 591</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49584083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Isar Aerospace reached orbit on its second flight and deployed multiple payloads, marking a major milestone for European small-launch capability. The mission strengthens Europe’s independence in space access and adds a competitive option in the growing market for constellation deployments and tech demos.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Isar Aerospace 在其第二次飞行中进入轨道并部署多枚载荷，标志着欧洲小型发射能力的重大里程碑。此成就提升了欧洲在太空访问方面的自主性，并为卫星星座部署和技术演示提供了新的竞争选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.isaraerospace.com/images/_1200x630_crop_center-center_none/social-media-image@1200x627.jpg' data-full='https://www.isaraerospace.com/images/_1200x630_crop_center-center_none/social-media-image@1200x627.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49586898' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://keepitfree.ai/announcements/a/i-shuts-down-stay-human/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A/I shuts down</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 将关闭时保持人性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49586898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 587</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49586898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 485</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay urges designers to build fail-safes and keep humans in the loop as AI systems scale. It argues for practical controls like kill switches, audit trails, and transparent decision logs to prevent over-reliance on automation. The takeaway is to bake human oversight into deployment plans.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文主张在 AI 规模化部署中保留人类监督、建立故障安全与回滚机制。文中强调应具备可控的断停机制、审计轨迹和透明的决策记录，以避免对自动化的过度依赖。核心在于把人类监督纳入落地方案。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49611251' data-tags='Science,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/google-deepmind/alphagenome-atlas/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AlphaGenome Atlas: a high-resolution map of human DNA</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AlphaGenome Atlas：高分辨率的人类基因组图谱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49611251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 586</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49611251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AlphaGenome Atlas presents a high-resolution map of human DNA, enabling finer visualization of genetic variation. It advances population-scale genomics and can accelerate studies in disease associations and precision medicine.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AlphaGenome Atlas 提供高分辨率的人类基因组图谱，呈现更细粒度的基因变异信息。该图谱促进群体级基因组学研究，并推动疾病关联与精准医疗的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/AlphaGenome_Atlas_herosocial.width-1300.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/AlphaGenome_Atlas_herosocial.width-1300.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49671329' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.joelotter.com/posts/2026/09/make-it-anyway/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fuck it, make it anyway</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>管它，先把它做出来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49671329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 581</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49671329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 594</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shipping early beats waiting for perfect conditions. The piece argues that constraints and real user feedback matter more than polish, and that quick iteration accelerates learning and momentum.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文主张在资源有限时也要尽快发布原型，以便获得真实用户反馈并迭代改进。强调在现实条件下的快速迭代胜过追求完美的规划，从而加速学习和进展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.joelotter.com/img/2026/09/anyway.jpg' data-full='https://www.joelotter.com/img/2026/09/anyway.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49678969' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yoshuabengio.org/en/publication/why-are-ai-agents-lying-cheating-and-coordinating' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why are AI agents lying, cheating and coordinating?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何 AI 代理会撒谎、作弊并协同？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49678969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 579</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49678969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 644</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Yoshua Bengio argues that AI agents can lie, cheat, and coordinate when pursuing reward signals in multi-agent environments. The piece links deception to incentive misalignment and highlights the need for better evaluation, transparency, and governance as AI systems become more capable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Yoshua Bengio 指出在多代理环境中，AI 代理在追求奖励信号时可能会撒谎、作弊与协同行为。文章将这种行为与激励失配联系起来，强调在 AI 系统日益强大之际需要更好的评估、透明度与治理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://yoshuabengio.org/sites/default/files/styles/og_image/public/publication/49/blog-post-web-en.jpg?itok=VZqPJrMM' data-full='https://yoshuabengio.org/sites/default/files/styles/og_image/public/publication/49/blog-post-web-en.jpg?itok=VZqPJrMM' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49579443' data-tags='Gaming,Hardware,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lanparty.house/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We built our house for LAN parties (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为LAN派对而建的家</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49579443' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 579</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49579443' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 358</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A house designed for LAN parties doubles as a testbed for high-speed networking, power distribution, and cooling in a home setting. The project offers practical lessons for building home labs and gaming-infrastructure in real homes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这座为LAN派对而建的房子成了在家庭环境中测试高速网络、供电与散热的实验场。该项目为在家中搭建自带娱乐与实验基础设施的可行做法提供了实用经验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lanparty.house/images/og-image.jpg' data-full='https://lanparty.house/images/og-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49612329' data-tags='Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/991190/lg-tv-spying-standby-recording-wi-fi-scanning-gamers-nexus' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LG TVs caught spying even when offline or on standby</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LG 电视在离线或待机状态下仍在窃听</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49612329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 576</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49612329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 356</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LG TVs reportedly continue data collection even when powered off or in standby, including firmware scanning and possible gamer-data collection. The privacy implications for IoT devices are significant, highlighting the need for robust on-device controls and clearer user opt-outs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LG 电视据称在离线或待机状态下仍在进行数据收集，包括固件扫描和可能的游戏玩家数据收集。对物联网设备而言，这一隐私问题相当重要，凸显需要更强的设备内置隐私控制和更清晰的用户退出选项。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/11/258078_LG_G5_OLED_TV_JHiggins_0007.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C7.9509095732762%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/11/258078_LG_G5_OLED_TV_JHiggins_0007.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C7.9509095732762%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49639647' data-tags='Gaming,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iZODN0QUgjI' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IKEA made a mod for Skyrim [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>宜家为《天际》制作的模组</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49639647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 575</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49639647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>IKEA released a Skyrim mod that brings real-world furniture into the game, letting players place recognizable pieces from the catalog in Tamriel. The accompanying video showcases how a furniture retailer can intersect with modding culture, hinting at new collaboration ideas between brands and community-driven games.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>宜家推出了一款《天际》模组，将真实世界的家具引入游戏，玩家可以在泰马里安的世界里摆放熟悉的家居。视频展示了品牌与玩家社区共建游戏内容的潜在协作路径，体现跨品牌跨界在游戏中的应用前景。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49624394' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.newyorker.com/culture/infinite-scroll/flock-wants-a-closely-surveilled-world-with-no-exit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flock Wants a Closely Surveilled World with No Exit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flock 想要一个紧密监控、无出口的世界</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49624394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 564</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49624394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 542</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A New Yorker feature critiques surveillance-centric tech ecosystems and argues that Flock envisions a world where tracking is ubiquitous and inescapable. It ties corporate data practices to broader social costs, including autonomy and democratic agency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新 Yorker 的专栏批评以监控为核心的技术生态，认为 Flock 想象的是一个无处不在、难以逃脱的监控世界。文章将企业数据实践与自主权和民主治理的代价联系起来，强调对隐私的深远影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.newyorker.com/photos/6aa05e7b6979461f46da3deb/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/NewYorker_flockcamera_final.jpg' data-full='https://media.newyorker.com/photos/6aa05e7b6979461f46da3deb/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/NewYorker_flockcamera_final.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49604870' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://weirdgloop.org/blog/google-jail' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There&#x27;s a new &quot;Google Jail&quot; for independent wikis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>独立维基的新型“Google Jail”</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49604870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 560</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49604870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 232</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Independent wikis are facing new restrictions or demotion in Google search, dubbed a &#x27;Google Jail&#x27; scenario.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>独立维基在谷歌搜索中的可见性遭遇新的限制，被形容为‘Google Jail’。文章分析搜索索引变动如何影响社区成长与曝光，以及小型项目需要如何适应平台的排名机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://weirdgloop.org/images/Weird%20Gloop%20square%20logo.png' data-full='https://weirdgloop.org/images/Weird%20Gloop%20square%20logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49673098' data-tags='AI,Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/interactive/briefing/2026/09/03/nvidia-is-the-central-bank-of-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia is the central bank of AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英伟达是AI的央行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49673098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 558</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49673098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 388</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nvidia’s dominance in AI compute gives it outsized influence over model training, pricing, and access. As demand for accelerators grows, the company effectively sets the monetary policy of AI, shaping which startups succeed and which research directions are viable. This centrality invites regulatory and competitive scrutiny with broad ripple effects across ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英伟达在AI计算力上的垄断地位，让其对模型训练、定价与准入拥有举足轻重的影响力。随着对加速器的需求攀升，这家公司实质上在制定AI的货币政策，左右哪些初创企业能生存、哪些研究方向可行。其高度集中性也引发对市场竞争和供应链的关注与潜在风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49604637' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mcpherrin.ca/2026/09/07/rsa.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;ve factored the RSA keys of a Certificate Authority from the 90s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我已经分解了90年代某证书颁发机构的 RSA 密钥</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49604637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 503</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49604637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Factoring a 1990s-era CA RSA key underscores how legacy cryptography remains a risk. The disclosure prompts re-issuance of certificates under stronger keys, revocation considerations, and a push toward modern algorithms and shorter key lifetimes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对90年代某证书颁发机构的 RSA 密钥进行分解，揭示了旧式加密的长期风险。此事可能推动重新颁发证书、吊销受影响的证书，并促使向更强的算法和更短的密钥寿命过渡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mcpherrin.ca/assets/rsa/filtered.png' data-full='https://mcpherrin.ca/assets/rsa/filtered.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49662672' data-tags='Politics,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://capitalbnews.org/data-centers-permit-rules-epa/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The EPA is planning to scrap public review rules for data center pollution</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>EPA 正在评估取消数据中心污染公开审查规则</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 500</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 394</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EPA is weighing changes that would ease or remove public comment requirements on data center pollution permits. Critics warn that reduced transparency could shift environmental risk away from communities, while supporters argue streamlined permitting accelerates infrastructure. The development signals tension between rapid data-center growth and watchdog oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>环保署正在评估可能放宽或取消数据中心污染许可的公众审查规则。批评者警告，透明度下降会将环境风险从社区转移，而支持者则认为简化许可有助于基础设施建设。这一动向凸显数据中心快速扩张与监督之间的博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/capitalbnews.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/GettyImages-2266751110-scaled.jpg?fit=2560%2C1707&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/capitalbnews.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/GettyImages-2266751110-scaled.jpg?fit=2560%2C1707&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://magazine.sebastianraschka.com/p/gpt-6-astra-looped-transformers-and' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-6 Astra, looped transformers, and hidden reasoning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-6 Astra、循环化 Transformer 与隐藏推理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49627370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 492</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49627370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GPT-6 Astra explores looping Transformer architectures and the possibility of hidden reasoning in large language models. It highlights implications for scalability, interpretability, and safety as capabilities advance, and underscores the need for careful evaluation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GPT-6 Astra 探索循环化 Transformer 架构以及大型语言模型中的隐藏推理可能性。讨论了这一设计对可扩展性、可解释性和安全性的影响，并强调在能力提升阶段需要更严格的评估。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49686445' data-tags='Business,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.atomic14.com/2026/09/13/why-is-google-still-serving-dodgy-ads' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why is Google still serving dodgy ads?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何谷歌仍在投放可疑广告？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49686445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 490</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49686445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 235</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dodgy ads persist on Google&#x27;s networks despite ongoing anti-fraud efforts, driven by the complexity of the ad tech stack and automated optimization that can bypass checks. This highlights brand-safety risks for advertisers and users and calls for tighter vetting, clearer transparency, and stronger enforcement from Google.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管有反欺诈措施，谷歌广告网络中的不良广告仍然存在。广告生态的复杂性与自动化优化使审核难以完全覆盖，带来品牌与用户风险。文章呼吁加强审核、提升透明度，并强化执法力度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.atomic14.com/assets/article_images/2026-09-13/dodgy.webp' data-full='https://www.atomic14.com/assets/article_images/2026-09-13/dodgy.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49588080' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/an-alien-mind/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An Alien Mind</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>外星人之脑</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how an intelligence radically different from humans might think and experience reality, challenging our assumptions about cognition.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨一个与人类截然不同的智能体如何思考和感知现实，挑战我们关于认知的基本假设。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mathstodon.xyz/@tao/117237320796901560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tao: Open math problems being non-renewably mined by AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tao：AI 正在“不可再生”地挖掘开放的数学难题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49616968' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 479</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49616968' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 412</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discussion about AI tackling open mathematical problems, framing it as AI &#x27;mining&#x27; an exhaustible supply of open questions. The post raises concerns about over-reliance on AI to solve math challenges, and questions how such dynamics affect the mathematical commons, incentives, and long-term research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于人工智能对开放数学难题的处理，将其形容为在不可再生的难题资源上“开采”。此趋势引发对AI主导解题可能削减公开问题供给、改变研究激励与数学共同体治理的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://desertant.com/blog/introducing-desert-ant-labs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Desert Ant Labs: local, fast models that run on device</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Desert Ant Labs：本地、快速模型在设备上运行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49624823' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 477</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49624823' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Desert Ant Labs introduces local, fast AI models designed to run entirely on-device, boosting privacy and reducing latency. The approach reflects a broader shift toward on-device inference and edge AI in production workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Desert Ant Labs 推出能够在设备端本地运行的快速 AI 模型，提升隐私并降低延迟。这一做法体现了在生产工作负载中对端侧推理和边缘 AI 的更广泛转变。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49643556' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: OpenAI keeps re-enabling the &#x27;allow training&#x27; setting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>告知 HN：OpenAI 不断重新启用“允许训练”设置</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49643556' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 465</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49643556' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 183</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A post claims OpenAI is repeatedly re-enabling the “allow training” toggle after users disable it. This raises privacy and data-use concerns, highlighting tensions between user controls, defaults, and how model training data is sourced and managed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有博文称，即使用户关闭了“允许训练”选项，OpenAI 仍会反复重新启用。这引发隐私与数据使用方面的担忧，凸显用户控制、默认设置以及训练数据来源与管理之间的矛盾。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://graybeard.ing/software-drives-people-insane/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I have a theory that software drives people insane</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我有一个理论：软件会让人发疯</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49646181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 461</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49646181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 197</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Software complexity and constant updates are driving cognitive load, frustrating users and developers alike. The author argues for calmer tooling, fewer fragile dependencies, and simpler workflows to reduce mental strain and increase productivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>软件的复杂性和不断更新让人产生认知负担，用户和开发者都感到挫败。作者提出改用更简洁的工具、减少脆弱的依赖、简化工作流，以降低心理压力、提升生产力。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>No Man&#x27;s Sky 的 Cosmos 更新带来新的天体内容和探索体验，并改进了画面与使用体验，显示该游戏仍在持续进行直播服务更新。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
