<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cel.cs.brown.edu/blog/design-space-async-await/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Design Space Exploration of Async/Await</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Async/Await 的设计空间探索</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49626718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 449</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49626718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 129</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Design Space Exploration of Async/Await surveys trade-offs between readability, stack traces, error handling, and performance across asynchronous patterns. It distills practical takeaways for writing robust, maintainable async code and when to prefer async/await vs. alternatives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Async/Await 的设计空间探索》梳理了在不同异步模式中可读性、堆栈追踪、错误处理与性能之间的权衡。给出编写健壮、可维护异步代码的实用要点，以及何时应选择 async/await 而非其他方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cel.cs.brown.edu/assets/cel-spaced-BGjllCyA.webp' data-full='https://cel.cs.brown.edu/assets/cel-spaced-BGjllCyA.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cognition unveils SWE-2, a new large-language model that rivals Fable 5.1 and GPT-Astra. The launch broadens the competitive options for enterprises exploring alternatives to OpenAI, potentially influencing pricing and deployment decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cognition 发布 SWE-2，宣称在性能上可与 Fable 5.1 与 GPT-Astra 比肩。此举扩大了企业对大模型的选择，可能影响定价与部署决策。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/are-self-driving-cars-safe' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Growing proof that autonomous cars save lives</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日益增多的证据表明自动驾驶汽车能拯救生命</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49629886' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 438</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49629886' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 841</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A growing body of real-world data indicates autonomous vehicles reduce crash rates and save lives as deployment expands. These findings bolster the case for automation while informing policy, infrastructure, and safety standards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>越来越多的实证数据表明，随着自动驾驶汽车的普及，其发生车祸的概率在下降，从而拯救生命。这些发现为自动化提供了更强的安全论据，并为政策、基础设施和安全标准提供参考。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nishantjosh.dev/blogs/how-to-build-a-fking-printer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to build a printer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何自行构建一台打印机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49617255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 437</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49617255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Offers a hands-on guide to building a printer from scratch, detailing mechanical design, electronics, and calibration steps. It demystifies the hardware, showing how open-source and modular components can yield a functional print engine. This is useful for hobbyists and educators aiming to customize printers or understand how consumer devices fit together.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供从零开始搭建打印机的实操指南，涵盖机械结构、电路与标定步骤。内容去神秘化硬件，展示如何用开源、模块化组件拼装出可用的打印系统。对爱好者与教育者定制打印机、理解消费级设备的工作原理很有帮助。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49654229' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/2026/9/7/astra-why/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Astra for Coding: Why Are We Doing This Again?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Astra 用于编码：我们为什么要再做这个？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49654229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 436</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49654229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 328</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Revisits the goals and tradeoffs behind Astra for Coding, challenging whether the project’s core assumptions still hold. It highlights lessons learned, what worked well, and what would have to change to keep moving forward.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>重新审视用于编码的 Astra 项目的目标与权衡，质疑核心假设是否仍然成立。文中总结了经验教训、哪些做得好，以及若要继续推进需要改变的地方。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这则关于盖茨尝试安装 2003 版《MovieMaker》的趣闻，折射出对早期消费级软件的怀旧情绪以及向后兼容性的挑战。即便是科技领袖也会遇到旧软件安装的摩擦，凸显了软件生态在演进过程中的不同侧面。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49624856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 423</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49624856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 256</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A researcher documents advertising malicious software on Google Ads to study platform vetting. The revelation reveals gaps in automated review and fraud detection, underscoring the need for stronger vetting and abuse controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者记录在 Google Ads 上投放恶意软件广告，以测试平台防护能力，揭示自动审核与防欺诈机制的不足。此事凸显加强广告体系审查和滥用检测的必要性。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>索尼网站对玩家在某些情境下“拥有”数字游戏的表述，触及数字权利中的所有权与许可之辩。此类细微表述可能影响相关诉讼与平台对用户权利的界定。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49598291' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/iczelia/bzip3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>bzip3</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>bzip3：新一代无损压缩格式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49598291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 416</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49598291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 120</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>bzip3 is a new lossless compression format designed to outperform existing algorithms like bz2 in speed and compression ratio. It aims to integrate with existing tooling and influence archival and software distribution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>bzip3 是一种新型无损压缩格式，目标是在速度与压缩比方面优于 bz2 等现有算法。它设计为兼容现有工具链，有望影响归档和软件分发的效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e958b5356233523c148a815dab659fb888de0d5f4c4ef022342f56fc2c1ae18b/iczelia/bzip3' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e958b5356233523c148a815dab659fb888de0d5f4c4ef022342f56fc2c1ae18b/iczelia/bzip3' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/117225539756835649' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GrapheneOS Overhauled Default Apps and Secure Clipboard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GrapheneOS 全面重构默认应用与安全剪贴板</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49590512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 405</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49590512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 257</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS has overhauled its default apps and added a secure clipboard feature. The changes reduce data leakage and tighten app sandboxing on its Android-based privacy-first platform. This move reinforces GrapheneOS&#x27;s focus on minimizing Google dependencies and improving user privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS 对默认应用进行了全面重构，并增强了安全剪贴板功能。这些改动降低数据泄露风险，强化了基于沙箱的应用隔离，契合其以隐私为先的操作系统定位。此举凸显其减少谷歌依赖、提升用户隐私的长期目标。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A satirical take on AI adoption, arguing that you must return to the office to use AI in person. It skewers workplace culture and the hype around AI by imagining in-office collaboration as a prerequisite for effective AI use.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49658299' data-tags='Politics,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/live/cmd683p01eljt' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Houthis &#x27;take control&#x27; of key island in global shipping route</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>胡塞武装夺取全球航运要道上的关键岛屿</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49658299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 382</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49658299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 676</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two days of shipping disruption rumors: The Houthis claim control of a key island along a major global trade route. If confirmed, this could reroute vessels, elevate insurance costs, and intensify geopolitical risk for energy and goods moving through the Red Sea.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道称胡塞武装声称控制全球航运要道上的一个关键岛屿，若属实将影响船舶通行、保险成本和能源/商品运输。地缘风险上升，全球供应链可能受到波及。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_news/1200/cpsprodpb/10fd/live/2250a8b0-adc5-11f1-bc1f-3f186ca4140c.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_news/1200/cpsprodpb/10fd/live/2250a8b0-adc5-11f1-bc1f-3f186ca4140c.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ciphercue.com/blog/european-cdn-concentration-cloudflare-nine-in-ten' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Among European Companies That Use a CDN, Nearly 9 in 10 Use Cloudflare</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在使用CDN的欧洲企业中，近九成依赖Cloudflare</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49607443' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 382</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49607443' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 372</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare dominates the European CDN landscape, signaling high vendor concentration as edge delivery becomes essential for digital services. This raises questions about resilience, privacy, and regulatory scrutiny when a single vendor handles a large share of traffic and security services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare在欧洲CDN领域占据主导地位，随着边缘交付成为数字服务的关键部分，市场高度集中引发对韧性、隐私与监管的关注。单一供应商对大量流量与安全服务的控制，可能带来潜在风险与依赖性问题。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Mexican student built an acoustic fire extinguisher that uses sound waves to suppress fires in seconds. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名墨西哥学生发明了一种声学灭火器，利用声波在几秒钟内扑灭火源。这一新颖、非接触式的灭火方法展示了潜在的安全和部署意义，但仍需大量现实世界测试与标准化。</p>

The update signals a continued push to weave visual reasoning into conversational AI for richer interactions.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techdirt.com/2026/09/08/paramount-caught-using-astroturf-group-to-drum-up-fake-support-for-merger/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Paramount Caught Using &#x27;Astroturf&#x27; Group to Drum Up Fake Support for Merger</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Paramount 被发现使用人造草根团体推动对并购的虚假支持</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49610900' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 377</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49610900' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 123</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Paramount reportedly used an astroturf group to drum up fake public support for a merger. The revelation raises concerns about PR manipulation and could invite regulatory scrutiny of media consolidation tactics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，Paramount 动用了人造草根团体为合并案制造虚假民意支持。这一曝光引发对公关操控的担忧，可能引来对媒体合并策略的监管审查。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spinoff.nasa.gov/Manipulating_Satellite_Photos_Now_Reveals_Ancient_Images' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Technique for Manipulating Satellite Photos Now Reveals Ancient Images (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>操控卫星照片的新技术现能揭示古代影像</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49645437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 373</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49645437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A NASA Spinoff piece explains how advanced satellite image processing can reveal ancient features hidden in archival imagery, from buried structures to past landscapes. The capability raises exciting historical insights, while also prompting considerations about data interpretation and consent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项 NASA Spinoff 报告描述，通过高级卫星影像处理，可以从存档影像中揭示隐藏的古代特征，如古代建筑和昔日地貌。这一能力带来有趣的历史线索，同时也引发对数据解读与使用边界的思考。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c8r6y4me2g6o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google will buy half the electricity from one of Finland&#x27;s nuclear power plants</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌将从芬兰一座核电厂购买一半电力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49652105' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 368</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49652105' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 334</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google will buy half of the electricity produced by a Finnish nuclear plant, illustrating large-scale corporate demand for clean power. The move signals growing corporate alignment on decarbonization and could influence Finland&#x27;s energy market and grid flexibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这显示出企业对清洁能源的大规模采购趋势，可能影响芬兰电力市场与电网的供需格局。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hachyderm.io/@simontatham/117201594980991062' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linux Zoom client proactively reading everything written to X11 clipboard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux Zoom 客户端主动读取 X11 剪贴板中的内容</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49675902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 366</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49675902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 137</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Linux Zoom client was found to proactively read the X11 clipboard, risking leakage of sensitive data across apps. The disclosure prompts calls for better UI sandboxing and privacy controls in cross-application interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>发现 Linux 版 Zoom 客户端会主动读取 X11 剪贴板中的内容，可能泄露跨应用的敏感数据。这一披露敦促加强跨应用交互中的 UI 沙箱和隐私控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hachyderm.io/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://hachyderm.io/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49640646' data-tags='Programming,Design,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/20260909-00/?p=112683' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What algorithm did Windows XP use to choose your initial user picture?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows XP 选择初始用户头像所用的算法是什么？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49640646' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 357</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49640646' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 177</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Windows XP didn&#x27;t pick the first user picture at random. The article explains the deterministic mapping from a user&#x27;s security identifier to one of a small set of default avatars, a design choice that kept the onboarding lightweight. This seed-based approach yields a reproducible UX with minimal overhead.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Windows XP 并非随机分配初始用户头像。文章揭示了如何使用用户的 SID/名称等身份信息，将其确定性地映射到一组内置头像中的一个，这种设计使新用户的初始体验简单高效。基于种子的一致性策略有助于以低开销实现可重复的用户体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/wp-content/uploads/sites/38/2019/02/ShowCover.jpg' data-full='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/wp-content/uploads/sites/38/2019/02/ShowCover.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49594251' data-tags='Open Source,Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://itsfoss.com/news/switzerland-replace-microssoft-pilot/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Switzerland&#x27;s Federal Government Is Replacing Microsoft on 3k Computers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>瑞士联邦政府用开源替代 Microsoft，覆盖约3000台电脑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49594251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 357</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49594251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 280</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Switzerland&#x27;s government is piloting a switch away from Microsoft on about 3,000 computers, likely to embrace open-source alternatives. The move aims to reduce vendor lock-in, improve security, and support a more transparent IT stack.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>瑞士联邦政府正在对约3000台计算机进行测试性替换，放弃对 Microsoft 的依赖，转向开源替代方案。此举旨在降低供应商锁定、提升安全性，并推动 IT 架构的透明化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://itsfoss.com/content/images/2026/09/swiss-federal-migration.webp' data-full='https://itsfoss.com/content/images/2026/09/swiss-federal-migration.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49590611' data-tags='Web,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://anubis.techaro.lol/blog/2026/anubis-wasm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It took a year to ship WebAssembly in Anubis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Anubis 上实现 WebAssembly 花了一整年</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49590611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 356</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49590611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It took a year to ship WebAssembly in Anubis, detailing the long arc of adding WASM support to their environment. The outcome illustrates how challenging language runtime features can be to integrate in a focused project.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Anubis 项目中实现 WebAssembly 的过程耗时整整一年，揭示将 WASM 集成到环境中的种种挑战。文章聚焦调试、兼容性和工具链等难题，解释了为何功能落地往往会被拖延。最终成果也显示了在聚焦项目中整合语言运行时特性的复杂性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/munJKF7iWMsWJLAH2/astra-and-fable-still-hack-on-simple-variants-of-alignment' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Astra and Fable still hack on simple variants of alignment evals from 2025</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Astra 与 Fable 继续改进基于 2025 年对齐评估的简单变体</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684393' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 350</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684393' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 167</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astra and Fable keep iterating on simple alignment eval variants first proposed in 2025, probing whether minor task changes expose alignment gaps in current models. The work highlights the fragility of current evals as AI systems scale and the challenge of measuring true alignment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Astra 与 Fable 继续在 2025 年提出的对齐评估的简单变体上进行试验，观察在任务设定微小变化下模型对齐性的表现。此举暴露当前对齐评估在规模化 AI 面前的脆弱性及测量真正对齐的难度。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A secretive DHS predictive policing unit is analyzing Americans&#x27; financial habits to flag &#x27;risk&#x27; and trigger enforcement actions. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个神秘的 DHS 预测性警务单位正在分析美国人的金融习惯以标记潜在风险并触发执法行动。报告凸显公民自由、数据治理方面的担忧，以及金融数据在警务中的潜在滥用风险。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI released the Agents API, enabling developers to build autonomous agents that plan, reason, and operate tools to tackle multi-step tasks. It streamlines AI-driven automation while raising practical considerations around safety, cost, and tool integration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 发布 Agents API，使开发者能够构建具备规划、推理和调用工具能力的自治代理，能执行多步任务。它降低了构建 AI 驱动自动化的门槛，同时需要关注安全性、成本与工具生态的整合等挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.claymath.org/news/navier-stokes-announcement/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Navier-Stokes Announcement</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Clay Mathematics Institute issues an official Navier–Stokes announcement, signaling new developments on the famous unsolved problem about the existence and smoothness of solutions. The note outlines status and potential progress, underscoring the ongoing interest from pure math and applied fluid dynamics communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>克雷数学研究所发布关于 Navier–Stokes 的官方公告，标志着关于解的存在性与光滑性这一著名未解问题的最新进展。公告概述了现状与潜在进展，强调纯数学与应用流体力学社区持续的关注。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.researchagenda.news/articles/the-waymo-effect.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Waymo effect: how AI is quietly making research less collaborative</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Waymo效应：AI正悄然削弱研究协作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49656496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 327</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49656496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 299</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Waymo effect argues that AI tools and proprietary research pipelines are quietly shrinking collaboration and openness in science. As AI centralizes problem-solving power, there are concerns about reproducibility and access, calling for open standards and governance to preserve shared progress.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Waymo效应指出AI工具和专有研究流程正在悄悄削弱科学研究中的协作与开放性。随着AI把问题解决权集中，需要开放标准与治理来维护共享的进步和可重复性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.researchagenda.news/assets/cards/the-waymo-effect-d66b2c0d.png' data-full='https://www.researchagenda.news/assets/cards/the-waymo-effect-d66b2c0d.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple unveils the iPhone Duo, a dual-screen device aimed at expanding multitasking and productivity. The form-factor shift signals Apple’s exploration of foldable dynamics and how software will adapt to a broader device canvas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果发布 iPhone Duo，这款双屏设备旨在提升多任务处理与生产力。此形态的探索表明苹果在折叠屏领域的尝试，以及软件如何适配更丰富的设备画布。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/09/11/y-combinators-garry-tan-wants-u-s-open-weight-ai-labs-to-distill-frontier-models-too/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Garry Tan wants US open-weight AI labs to &#x27;distill&#x27; frontier models, too</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Garry Tan 希望美国公开权重的 AI 实验室也参与对前沿模型进行蒸馏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49685253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 322</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49685253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 166</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Garry Tan argues that US open-weight AI labs should also distill frontier models to improve safety and accessibility, not just large incumbents. The idea envisions a more transparent, collaborative ecosystem around powerful AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Garry Tan 提议美国开放权重的 AI 实验室也应对前沿模型进行蒸馏，以提升安全性和可访问性，推动更为透明和协作的 AI 生态。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://austinhenley.com/blog/python1024.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making a Python interpreter in 1024 bytes</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fable 5.1 has cracked the Cyphral Distich, a cipher dating back three centuries. The reveal underscores how modern cryptographic tooling and collaborative puzzle solving can crack even long-standing cryptographic curiosities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fable 5.1 终于破解了370年前的 Cyphral Distich 密码。此成就凸显现代密码学工具的成熟，以及长期未解之谜对密码学爱好者的持续吸引力。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS has released a rewritten Messages app built for privacy-first Android environments, removing reliance on Google&#x27;s default messaging stack. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS 已发布面向隐私优先 Android 环境的重写 Messages 应用，摆脱对 Google 默认短信栈的依赖。此举凸显专注隐私的 ROM 如何通过开源实现更安全的端到端消息传输。对于寻求独立、透明隐私保护的移动用户而言，这是一个值得关注的进展。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hitachi launches CO2-based heat-pump water heaters with tariff controls tailored to solar billing, promoting energy efficiency and grid-friendly operation for solar homes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日立推出以 CO2 为工质的热泵热水器，新增面向太阳能 tariff 的控制，推动节能与更好地适应分布式光伏家庭的用能模式。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Overview of the Logo language, its turtle graphics and education-focused design; notes on how it influenced teaching programming and early interactive environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 Logo 编程语言及其海龟图形和面向教育的设计，阐述它如何影响了编程教学和早期交互式环境的发展。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://musictheory.pugetsound.edu/mt21c/MusicTheory.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Music Theory for the 21st-Century Classroom</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>21世纪课堂的音乐理论</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49647134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 296</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49647134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>To keep music theory relevant in the digital era, the guide envisions a 21st-century classroom that blends software, data-driven feedback, and inclusive pedagogy. It outlines practical approaches—interactive exercises, AI-assisted analysis, and collaboration—that make theory accessible to diverse learners.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为了让音乐理论在数字时代保持相关性，本文倡议21世纪课堂应结合软件、数据驱动的反馈和包容性教学。通过互动练习、AI 辅助分析和协作等方式，使理论对不同学习者都易于接受。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/personal-tech/2026/09/07/smartphone-makers-dont-bother-to-comply-with-eu-repairability-requirements/5294532' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Smartphone makers don&#x27;t bother to comply with EU repairability requirements</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>智能手机厂商不遵守欧盟可修复性要求</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49597189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 296</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49597189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 198</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU repairability requirements aim to extend device lifespans, but many smartphone makers appear to resist compliance. The result could worsen e-waste and consumer frustration, highlighting gaps in enforcement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟的可修复性要求旨在延长设备寿命，但多家手机厂商似乎并不积极遵守。此举可能导致更多电子废物与消费者维权难题，同时凸显执法力度的不足。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/4093968.jpg?imageId=4093968&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/4093968.jpg?imageId=4093968&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/ValerioCapraro/status/2097791836269977996' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI might have stolen another major proof</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 可能又窃取了一项重大证明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49638353' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 294</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49638353' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Raises questions about provenance and intellectual property in AI research, suggesting that a major proof may have been claimed or used without proper attribution. The note calls for scrutiny of sources and claims in AI math.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>质疑人工智能研究中的来源与署名问题，暗示一项重要证明可能被错误归属或使用，呼吁对 AI 数学领域的来源与主张进行严格核查。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ToP9xfLDSME' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LG Says We&#x27;re Fake News [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LG：我们是假新闻[视频]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49676324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 291</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49676324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 149</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A video captures LG asserting that coverage about them is fake news, highlighting the tension between media narratives and corporate messaging online. The clip underscores the friction brands face in the digital age when dealing with press and social video.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一段视频显示 LG 声称相关报道是假新闻，凸显媒体叙事与企业信息在网络时代的张力。该剪辑反映品牌在面对新闻报道和社交视频时所面临的博弈。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49676085' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jacob.gold/posts/open-letter-to-dario-amodei-about-open-weights/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An open letter to Dario: if you mean it, open the weights</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>致Dario的一封公开信：若你认真的，就开放权重</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49676085' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 291</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49676085' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open letter to Dario Amodei urges releasing model weights, arguing that openness accelerates safety research and community verification. It frames open weights as a practical step toward better alignment and accountability in AI development, not a threat. The letter amplifies a central fault line in AI: openness versus safeguarding proprietary progress.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>给 Dario Amodei 的公开信呼吁公开模型权重，主张开放有助于加速安全研究和社区验证。信中将权重开放视为提升对齐和问责的切实之举，而非威胁。此议题凸显了 AI 领域在开放与保护知识产权之间的核心分歧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jacob.gold/images/open-letter-to-dario-amodei-about-open-weights.webp' data-full='https://jacob.gold/images/open-letter-to-dario-amodei-about-open-weights.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A discussion about whether to publish results from experiments where an AI tool interacts with a piano, touching on ownership, safety, and disclosure norms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论在让 AI 工具与钢琴互动的实验结果是否应公开，涉及版权、安全与信息披露等伦理与规范问题。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49619848' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dunstanorchard.com/apple-ipod-engraver/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple iPod Engraver (2019)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果 iPod 刻字服务（2019）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49619848' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 283</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49619848' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple offered an engraving option on iPods in 2019, allowing customers to personalize devices with names or messages. The feature highlights how small customization options can boost gift appeal and perceived value while revealing the practical costs and workflows behind personalization at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果在2019年为 iPod 提供了刻字服务，允许用户个性化设备。这个小小的定制功能体现了个性化如何提升礼品吸引力和附加值，同时揭示了规模化定制背后的实际成本和工作流程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dunstanorchard.com/apple-ipod-engraver/og.jpg' data-full='https://dunstanorchard.com/apple-ipod-engraver/og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49630566' data-tags='Hardware,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/09/introducing-apple-watch-series-12-with-the-all-new-health-sensing-system/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple Watch Series 12</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Apple Watch Series 12 全新健康感应系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49630566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 280</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49630566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 381</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple Watch Series 12 debuts with a reimagined health sensing system, expanding metrics and proactive health insights. The upgrade likely includes more sensors and tighter integration with the Health app and watchOS features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Apple Watch Series 12 亮相，搭载全新健康感应系统，扩展健康指标并提供更主动的健康洞察。新传感器与 Health 应用和 watchOS 的深度整合，预计带来更全面的健康监测。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/09/introducing-apple-watch-series-12-with-the-all-new-health-sensing-system/tile/Apple-Watch-Series-12-2up-260909-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202609091909' data-full='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/09/introducing-apple-watch-series-12-with-the-all-new-health-sensing-system/tile/Apple-Watch-Series-12-2up-260909-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202609091909' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49611128' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://quesma.com/blog/qwen38-27b-quantizations-benchmarked/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Benchmarking Qwen3.8 27B quantizations: 4-bit holds up, 1-bit collapses</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对 Qwen3.8 27B 量化的基准测试：4 位保持性能，1 位崩溃</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49611128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 279</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49611128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 129</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New benchmarks show that 4-bit quantization for Qwen3.8 27B preserves accuracy and usability on constrained hardware, while 1-bit quantization collapses performance severely. The results underscore the trade-offs between model size, speed, and fidelity for deploying large LLMs in resource-limited environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新基准显示，Qwen3.8 27B 的 4 位量化在受限硬件上仍能维持准确性和可用性，而 1 位量化则性能严重下降。结果强调在资源受限环境中部署大模型时，模型规模、速度与保真度之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://quesma.com/_astro/thumbnail.B4Hy1R5v.png' data-full='https://quesma.com/_astro/thumbnail.B4Hy1R5v.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49616257' data-tags='Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/argonautlabsai/deltafin' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kimi K3 (2.8T) at 1 token/s on a MacBook Pro, streamed from four SSDs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kimi K3（2.8T）在 MacBook Pro 上以每秒 1 個 token，來自四個 SSD 的流式推理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49616257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 277</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49616257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Kimi K3 (2.8T) runs at 1 token per second on a MacBook Pro, streaming from four SSDs. This demonstrates how high-throughput storage and portable hardware can sustain on-device inference for large models, signaling practical paths for mobile AI experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kimi K3（2.8T）在 MacBook Pro 上以每秒 1 個 token 的速度推理，數據由四個 SSD 流式提供。此展現顯示高帶寬存儲與便攜硬件可支撐大模型的本地推理，為移動端 AI 實驗提供實用路徑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c0b9fa39b467311d003e5ba44f3c757768c2a04b02a0550278d9ae69be4614d0/argonautlabsai/deltafin' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c0b9fa39b467311d003e5ba44f3c757768c2a04b02a0550278d9ae69be4614d0/argonautlabsai/deltafin' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49645686' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://planetscale.com/blog/introducing-neki' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Neki – Sharded Postgres</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Neki：分片 Postgres</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49645686' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 275</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49645686' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Neki is Planetscale&#x27;s sharded PostgreSQL solution, designed to scale writes and workloads across multiple shards with consistent behavior. It signals a practical path toward large-scale multi-tenant databases and better horizontal scalability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Neki 是 Planetscale 的分片 Postgres 方案，旨在跨多个分片实现可伸缩写入与工作负载，并维持一致性。它为大规模多租户数据库提供现实的水平扩展路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://planetscale.com/assets/introducing-neki-social-O6Jxs-bC.png' data-full='https://planetscale.com/assets/introducing-neki-social-O6Jxs-bC.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49658311' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://earendil.com/posts/measuring-code-sloppiness/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Measuring the sloppiness of code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>衡量代码的马虎度</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49658311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 271</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49658311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 231</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proposes metrics to quantify code sloppiness beyond line counts—capturing maintenance friction, architectural debt, and testability. The piece argues that formal measurements can guide refactoring and better engineering practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出超越行数的量化代码马虎度的指标，涵盖维护摩擦、架构债务和可测试性等维度。主张通过正式的度量来指导重构与改进工程实践。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://earendil.com/static/og/posts/measuring-code-sloppiness.png' data-full='https://earendil.com/static/og/posts/measuring-code-sloppiness.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49589003' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/zedeus/nitter' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Following legal advice, the Nitter project will continue</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在法律意见指引下，Nitter 项目将继续</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49589003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 269</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49589003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nitter&#x27;s maintainers say they will continue developing the privacy-respecting Twitter front-end after receiving legal counsel. The move highlights ongoing tension between open-source Twitter front-ends and platform policies, and how developers navigate compliance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nitter 的维护者表示在获得法律意见后将继续开发。此举凸显开源 Twitter 前端与平台政策之间持续的紧张关系，以及开发者在合规性方面的取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3ec0361757be9a4b1d69853b22c0118a1eb8e41fc5180e8256bc16cb08b9dbb7/zedeus/nitter' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3ec0361757be9a4b1d69853b22c0118a1eb8e41fc5180e8256bc16cb08b9dbb7/zedeus/nitter' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49649124' data-tags='Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://auberon.xyz/blog/posts/deathray/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Deathray: A simple way for an untrusted site to freeze a Mac</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Deathray：让不可信站点冻结 Mac 的简易方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49649124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 268</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49649124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 194</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Deathray demonstrates a straightforward technique by which a hostile web page can cause a Mac to become unresponsive, leveraging a vulnerability or resource contention. The result underscores the fragility of system/browser isolation and the need for robust sandboxing and watchdogs to prevent cross-tab denial-of-service.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Deathray 展示了一个简单的技巧，利用不可信网页让 Mac 系统陷入无响应，可能通过资源耗尽或系统组件中的漏洞实现。该现象凸显了系统与浏览器在隔离与保护方面的脆弱性，需要更强的沙箱和监控机制来防止跨标签页的拒绝服务。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://auberon.xyz/images/liLogo.png' data-full='https://auberon.xyz/images/liLogo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49638280' data-tags='Culture,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mathstodon.xyz/@tao/117244102901892965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>All grown-ups were once children, but only few of them remember it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>成年人曾经都是孩子……却很少有人记得</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49638280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 267</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49638280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 231</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflective note on how adults forget the curiosity of childhood, urging readers to preserve a beginner&#x27;s mindset when tackling problems. It argues that maintaining that sense of wonder is crucial for learning and innovation in math, programming, and technology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇反思文章指出成年人常忘记童年的好奇心，呼吁在解决问题和学习新知识时保持初学者心态。强调持续保持好奇与谦逊有助于在数学、编程与科技创新中保持敏锐与创造力。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49659245' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://huggingface.co/security.txt' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>HuggingFace: Security.txt</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>HuggingFace 的 security.txt</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49659245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 266</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49659245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HuggingFace adopts the security.txt standard to publish security contact details and disclosure policy, making it easier for researchers to report vulnerabilities. This aligns with industry best practices and helps shorten response times while boosting security posture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HuggingFace 采用 security.txt 标准，公开安全联系信息和披露策略，方便研究人员报告漏洞。此举符合行业最佳实践，提升响应速度与安全治理水平。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49596055' data-tags='Programming,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mathathonchallenge.com/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Caltech Mathathon – first hackathon ever devoted to research level mathematics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加州理工学院数学马拉松：首个面向研究级数学的黑客松</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49596055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 265</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49596055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Caltech hosts the Mathathon, the first hackathon dedicated to research-level mathematics. Teams tackle open problems, blending coding, math, and visualization; could foster new tools and collaborative approaches in mathematical research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州理工学院举办数学马拉松，成为首个面向研究级数学的黑客松。参赛团队结合编程、数学与可视化解决开放性难题，推动数学研究的新工具与合作方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mathathonchallenge.com/img/mathathon-logo.png' data-full='https://mathathonchallenge.com/img/mathathon-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49602699' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.virtualizationhowto.com/2026/09/leaving-vmware-just-got-harder-after-broadcom-pulled-vddk-downloads/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Leaving VMware just got harder after Broadcom pulled VDDK downloads</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Broadcom 下架 VDDK 下载后，离开 VMware 更难</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49602699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 264</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49602699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Broadcom has pulled down VMware VDDK downloads, complicating exit strategies for customers looking to move away from VMware. VDDK (Virtual Disk Development Kit) underpins many backup, migration, and management tools, and losing easy access raises the cost and friction of departing a VMware-centric stack. Expect longer migration timelines and potential reevaluation of vendor dependencies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Broadcom 下架 VDDK 下载后，退出 VMware 的难度进一步上升。VDDK 是与 VMware 集成的关键工具之一，此举可能提高迁移成本并增加对原厂工具的依赖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.virtualizationhowto.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/vmware-vddk-downloads.png' data-full='https://www.virtualizationhowto.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/vmware-vddk-downloads.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49599992' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stockfishchess.org/blog/2026/stockfish-19/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stockfish 19</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Stockfish 19 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49599992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 261</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49599992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stockfish 19 arrives with improvements to evaluation and search that push the open-source engine&#x27;s standing in modern chess. The release also includes tuning and bug fixes that enhance strength across positions and hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Stockfish 19 发布，提升了评估与搜索能力，继续巩固其在现代国际象棋中的领先地位。此次版本带来多项调优和修复，使在各种局面和硬件上表现更强。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stockfishchess.org/images/blog/sf19.webp' data-full='https://stockfishchess.org/images/blog/sf19.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49683953' data-tags='Data,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/column/994172/your-car-is-selling-your-data' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Data collected by cars and sold to third parties</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>汽车数据被收集并出售给第三方</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49683953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 261</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49683953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Modern cars continuously collect telemetry, location, and sensor data, much of which is sold or shared with third parties. This raises privacy concerns and highlights who controls the data and how opt-outs are handled.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>现代汽车会持续收集遥测、位置信息和传感器数据，且其中相当一部分被出售或分享给第三方。这引发隐私担忧，同时暴露了数据的控制权以及退出机制的不足。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/09/gettyimages-144097019.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.678370919471%2C100%2C78.643258161058&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/09/gettyimages-144097019.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.678370919471%2C100%2C78.643258161058&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49643112' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bsky.app/profile/did:plc:ckaz32jwl6t2cno6fmuw2nhn/post/3mv4mt4ikss2d' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Another researcher says OpenAI trained on conversations, then claimed breakthrou</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>又有研究者称 OpenAI 曾在对话数据上训练模型，随后声称突破</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49643112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 259</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49643112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Another researcher alleges OpenAI trained its models on conversation data and later claimed a breakthrough. The claims amplify debates over data provenance, consent, and transparency in AI development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>另一位研究者指控 OpenAI 曾在对话数据上训练模型，随后再声称取得突破。这些说法加剧了关于数据来源、同意与透明度的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.bsky.app/img/feed_thumbnail/plain/did:plc:ckaz32jwl6t2cno6fmuw2nhn/bafkreibhtbxgedebzjj2iodupc357i4nv6aonabqbhysv4t2qrmiwhxvse' data-full='https://cdn.bsky.app/img/feed_thumbnail/plain/did:plc:ckaz32jwl6t2cno6fmuw2nhn/bafkreibhtbxgedebzjj2iodupc357i4nv6aonabqbhysv4t2qrmiwhxvse' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49676820' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://withspecific.com/benchmarks/real-swe' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Real-SWE: Benchmarking AI models on private, real-world, enterprise codebases</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Real-SWE：在私有真实企业代码库上对 AI 模型的基准测试</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49676820' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49676820' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Real-SWE benchmarks AI models directly on private, real-world enterprise codebases to assess performance and usefulness in practical coding tasks. The results reveal gaps between public benchmarks and production code, and emphasize privacy-preserving evaluation when assessing enterprise-grade AI tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Real-SWE 在私有且真实的企业代码库上对 AI 模型进行基准测试，以评估其在实际编码任务中的表现和实用性。结果揭示公开基准与生产代码之间的差距，并强调在评估企业级 AI 工具时对隐私保护的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://withspecific.com/_next/image?url=%2Fspecific-logo.png&amp;w=1920&amp;q=75&amp;dpl=dpl_ABGtodWDkmm7BqTikqe2cpVTakLw' data-full='https://withspecific.com/_next/image?url=%2Fspecific-logo.png&amp;w=1920&amp;q=75&amp;dpl=dpl_ABGtodWDkmm7BqTikqe2cpVTakLw' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/ID_AA_Carmack/status/2098443262214230095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t be the out of touch Kung Fu master</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>别做脱离现实的功夫大师</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49677577' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 248</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49677577' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 373</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A tongue-in-cheek warning against tech leaders who pretend to be wise but ignore practical realities; stay grounded, curious, and connected to real users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以调侃语气警示那些自以为高深却脱离实际的技术领导者，强调要脚踏实地、保持好奇心并与真实用户保持联系。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49558685' data-tags='Design,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jyn.dev/simple-is-not-the-same-as-small/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Simple Is Not Small</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>简单≠小</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49558685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 246</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49558685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Simplicity in design or API does not automatically translate to a small binary, low resource use, or minimal operational surface; the trade-offs that yield simple interfaces can hide complexity or bloat elsewhere. Effective engineering requires naming which constraints matter — simplicity for users, smallness for embedded targets, or minimal attack surface — and optimizing explicitly for that goal.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>设计或接口的简单性并不等同于二进制小、资源占用低或运行时开销小；为用户提供简单体验的权衡可能会把复杂性或膨胀转移到别处。工程实践需要明确哪种约束更重要——用户简洁、嵌入式的小体积或最小攻击面——并有针对性地优化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jyn.dev/assets/rope.png' data-full='https://jyn.dev/assets/rope.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Blizzard workers win a historic union contract, marking a potential turning point for collective bargaining in the game industry. The deal covers wages, healthcare, and working conditions, signaling growing union activity in the game industry and could influence other studios.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>暴雪员工赢得具有历史意义的工会合同，可能成为游戏行业集体谈判的转折点。合同涵盖薪资、医疗保障与工作条件等，显示出行业工会活动的上升趋势，或对其他工作室产生示范效应。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Engaging in hands-on activities yields tangible health and cognitive benefits within weeks, from mood boosts to increased resilience and activity. The piece argues that tactile, messy work can enhance learning and well-being rather than hinder it.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>动手实践在数周内就能带来明显的健康与认知收益，包括情绪提升、韧性增强与身体活动增加。文章指出，动手的实际操作有助于学习与身心健康，而非削弱它们。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49625056' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(140) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/SubscriberLink/1091015/d9e48318ed242b41/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stabilizing Rust&#x27;s Never Type</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>稳定化 Rust 的 Never 类型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49625056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 242</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49625056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rust&#x27;s Never type, denoted by !, represents a computation that never returns. Stabilization could simplify error handling and flow control for libraries and developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rust 的 Never 类型用 ! 表示永不返回的计算。文章讨论在稳定版本中稳定使用该类型的做法，以使非终止路径的表达更清晰并提升 API 的易用性。稳定化有望简化库与开发者的错误处理与流程控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://signoregalilei.com/2026/08/30/the-two-christian-saints-who-are-secretly-the-buddha/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The two Christian saints who are the Buddha</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>两位基督教圣人其实是佛陀</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49611051' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 242</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49611051' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 190</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay explores how two Christian saints can be read as Buddhist archetypes, inviting comparisons across faiths. It challenges rigid religious boundaries and highlights shared themes of compassion and wisdom.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨两位基督教圣人如何被理解为佛教的原型，邀请跨信仰的比较。它挑战僵化的宗教边界，强调同情与智慧等跨宗教的共同主题。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/TechEmails/status/2099214399840059428' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mark Zuckerberg: &quot;Cambridge Analytica&quot; (2017)</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49688157' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 240</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49688157' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zuckerberg faced congressional scrutiny in 2017 over Cambridge Analytica, highlighting how data practices intersect with privacy, platform governance, and regulatory pressure. The moment reshaped public discourse on Facebook’s business model.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2017 年，扎克伯格因剑桥分析事件接受多场听证，暴露了数据治理的脆弱性以及以数据为核心的商业模式。此后对 Facebook 的隐私议题与政策应对产生深远影响。</p>
