<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eiln.github.io/posts/ane.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Retrospectively Reverse-Engineering Apple&#x27;s Neural Engine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>事后对苹果神经引擎的逆向工程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49670032' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 231</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49670032' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective look at reverse-engineering Apple&#x27;s Neural Engine to map its core acceleration blocks and data paths. The findings highlight the trade-offs in closed-hardware AI design that affect performance portability across devices and raise questions about transparency. The work adds context to how software teams optimize ML workloads on Apple silicon today.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对苹果神经引擎进行事后逆向分析，揭示其核心加速模块和数据路径的特征。研究揭示了在封闭硬件上优化 AI 的取舍及其对跨设备性能迁移的影响，并引发对硬件加速器透明度的讨论。此类工作有助于理解在 Apple Silicon 上优化 ML 工作负载的现实挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://eiln.github.io/posts/2026-08-10-ane-cnn/artifacts/m1-die.png' data-full='https://eiln.github.io/posts/2026-08-10-ane-cnn/artifacts/m1-die.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49655286' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://www.iaea.org/newscenter/news/what-is-cherenkov-radiation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cherenkov Radiation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>切伦科夫辐射：介质中的超光速现象</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49655286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49655286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 101</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Charged particles traveling faster than light in a medium emit Cherenkov radiation, producing a characteristic blue glow. This effect is a practical tool in nuclear and particle physics, used in detectors like water Cherenkov arrays to identify high-energy particles, while respecting relativity. It also serves as a diagnostic signal in reactors and astrophysical experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>切伦科夫辐射是在粒子穿过介质时速度超过介质中的光速而发出的蓝光。它被广泛用于核与粒子物理实验的探测，例如水中切伦科夫探测器，用来识别高能粒子，同时遵守相对论。该现象也为反应堆监测和天体物理观测提供诊断性信号。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纳维-斯托克斯千禧年奖题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49621697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49621697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A piece reflects on the unsolved Navier–Stokes Millennium Prize Problem, explaining why proving existence and smoothness of solutions remains a monumental challenge. It considers what a resolution would mean for mathematics, physics, and computational science.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回顾未解的纳维-斯托克斯千禧年奖题，解释为何证明解的存在性和光滑性仍是一规模巨大的挑战。解题将对数学、物理和计算科学产生深远影响。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ox.ac.uk/news/2026-08-28-scientists-observe-einsteins-gravity-in-the-quantum-world' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scientists observe Einstein&#x27;s gravity in the quantum world</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科学家在量子世界观察到爱因斯坦引力效应</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49569838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 225</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49569838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate gravitational effects consistent with general relativity within a quantum system, bridging quantum mechanics and gravity. This experimental step sharpens questions about quantum gravity and how spacetime might behave at small scales.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者在量子系统中观测到符合广义相对论的引力效应， bridging 量子力学与引力之间的关系。这一实验性进展提出关于量子引力以及时空在微观尺度上如何运作的关键问题。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/institute/econ-scenarios' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What will our economic future look like?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们的经济未来会是什么样子？</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic&#x27;s institute presents scenarios on the economic future shaped by AI: productivity gains, job displacement, policy responses, and liquidity effects. The report helps frame long-run planning for technologists and policymakers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 研究院提出受 AI 影响的经济未来情景，包括生产率提升、就业替代、政策应对与流动性效应等，为科技界和政策制定者提供长期规划的框架。</p>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/DavidSacks/status/2098973625252708460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>David Sacks: OpenAI and Anthropic Don&#x27;t Need Regulations to Pace Frontier Models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>David Sacks：OpenAI 与 Anthropic 不需要监管就能推动前沿模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49685991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 223</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49685991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 165</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>David Sacks argues that regulation isn’t necessary to pace frontier AI models, suggesting market dynamics or internal governance could suffice. His stance adds fuel to the ongoing AI governance debate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大卫·萨克斯认为政府监管并非加速前沿 AI 模型所必需，市场、行业自律也能推动进展，这一观点在 AI 治理辩论中具有争议性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.ericgoldman.org/archives/2026/09/tweet-and-the-bird-logo-apparently-enter-the-public-domain-but-x-maintains-its-grip-on-the-twitter-mark-for-now-x-v-project-bluebird.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>“Tweet” and the bird logo apparently enter the public domain</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A legal tug-of-war suggests the word &#x27;tweet&#x27; and Twitter&#x27;s bird logo may enter the public domain, potentially loosening brand protections. The piece notes how a public-domain status could affect licensing, trademarks, and platform branding—and how the holder responds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道讨论“Tweet”以及鸟标志可能进入公有领域的法律进展，以及这将对商标、品牌策略和许可方式产生的潜在影响。文章也提及公司在此情形下的应对姿态。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/how-ai-math-breakthrough-ignited-controversy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How An AI math breakthrough ignited a controversy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 数学突破引发争议</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49624163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 220</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49624163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 229</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A breakthrough in AI-assisted mathematics catalyzed a heated debate over its validity and implications. The piece lays out what was claimed, why skeptics doubt the result, and what the controversy signals for future AI-aided theorem proving.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 辅助数学取得的突破引发激烈争议，核心在于结果的可靠性和方法透明度。文章分析争论点、不同观点，以及这一事件对未来 AI 辅助定理证明的潜在影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49662020' data-tags='Science,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/mind-altering-drugs-played-key-role-rise-andean-civilization' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Psychoactive substances helped spur Andean civilization</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>精神活性物质推动了安第斯文明的崛起</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 215</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Psychoactive substances are argued to have helped spur Andean civilization by facilitating ritual practice, social bonding, and large-scale coordination. The findings suggest mind-altering drugs played a role in the rise of complex societies alongside agriculture and trade. The work reframes how we understand the drivers of civilization in the Andes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究指出安第斯文明的兴起与精神活性物质的使用有关，助力仪式、社会凝聚和大规模动员。相关证据显示，致幻药在社会组织、贸易网络和治理结构的形成中发挥了作用。此研究改变了我们对安第斯文明崛起驱动力的认识。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49575515' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24888' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trusting-Trust Attack against an Entire Linux Distribution</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>针对整个 Linux 发行版的信任-信任攻击</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49575515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 213</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49575515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate a Trusting-Trust style attack that could compromise every package in a Linux distribution, revealing how supply chain trust scales to an OS. The work amplifies needs for reproducible builds, verifiable toolchains, and better supply-chain defenses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者展示一种信任-信任攻击，理论上可能破坏 Linux 发行版中的每个软件包，凸显供应链信任在整个操作系统层面的脆弱性。该工作推动对可复现构建、可验证工具链以及更完善的供应链防御的重视。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Read the Docs recently faced a large DDoS attack, disrupting access to hosted documentation. The incident sheds light on the resilience of open-source documentation platforms and the importance of robust mitigations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Read the Docs 最近遭遇大规模 DDoS 攻击，导致文档托管服务访问中断。事件凸显开源文档平台的韧性和需要强健的缓解措施。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49645907' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49645907' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical remote code execution vulnerability affects Forgejo up to version 16.0.3; upgrading to 16.0.4 or applying patches is advised. This underscores the ongoing need to monitor and patch self-hosted software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Forgejo 16.0.3 及以下版本存在严重远程代码执行漏洞，建议升级至 16.0.4 或应用补丁，凸显自托管软件的持续安全挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eiln.github.io/posts/ane-dma.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Getting 50 GB/S Back from the Apple Neural Engine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从 Apple Neural Engine 拿回 50 GB/s 带宽</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49636479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49636479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It underscores how hardware-software co-design can unlock throughput in AI accelerators on consumer silicon, with implications for performance-critical workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨通过优化内存访问或 DMA 路径，从 Apple Neural Engine 回收 50 GB/s 的带宽。此举凸显软硬件协同设计在释放消费级芯片上 AI 加速器吞吐量方面的重要性，对性能敏感的工作负载具有意义。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49665502' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://supuk.ch/papers/android-natt-keepalive-vpn-bypass' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Android NAT-T keepalive offload bypasses VPN lockdown</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Android NAT-T 保活离载绕过，突破 VPN 封锁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49665502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 201</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49665502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A technical paper shows an Android NAT-T keepalive offload can bypass VPN lockdown, potentially letting traffic escape enforced VPNs. The finding raises enterprise security concerns and the risk of data leakage on managed devices. Mitigations include disabling the offload or applying vendor patches, with policy implications for BYOD and MDM programs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份技术论文揭示 Android 的 NAT-T 保活离载可能绕过 VPN 封锁，导致流量绕出强制 VPN 的保护。此发现提升了企业安全风险，可能造成数据泄露，尤其在受控设备环境中。缓解措施包括禁用此离载或应用厂商补丁，并对 BYOD/MDM 策略产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://supuk.ch/images/headers/android-natt-keepalive-post.jpg' data-full='https://supuk.ch/images/headers/android-natt-keepalive-post.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49659647' data-tags='Show HN,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hcker.news/?ai=exclude' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Hacker News, without AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Hacker News（无 AI）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49659647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 200</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49659647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 88</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An independent Show HN post presents a Hacker News instance stripped of AI features, highlighting a human-curated feed. It sparks discussion about the value of transparent, AI-free content ranking and moderation versus AI-assisted systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一则独立的 Show HN 提交展示了一个去除 AI 功能的 Hacker News 版本，强调人类筛选的信息流。引发关于透明、无 AI 的内容排序与治理与 AI 辅助系统之间取舍的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openreview.net/challenge?redirect=%2Fforum%3Fid%3Dpc7fqaOcAH' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Large language models develop novel social biases through adaptive exploration</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型语言模型在自适应探索中产生新型社会偏见</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49617581' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49617581' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Adaptive exploration in large language models can surface novel social biases as models adjust to prompts and tasks. The finding suggests bias is not static but shaped by the model&#x27;s search strategy, with implications for safety, evaluation, and debiasing efforts. The takeaway is to design exploration and prompting policies that minimize biased behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在自适应探索任务空间时，模型输出会显现、甚至改变偏见信号。研究表明偏见并非一成不变，而受探索策略影响，安全与公平评估需将探索动态纳入考量。核心要点是通过审慎设计探索和提示策略来降低偏见风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/20260910-00/?p=112689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why is the x86 undefined instruction called ud2? Why 2?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么 x86 的未定义指令叫 ud2？为什么是 2？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49683262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 186</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49683262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post explains the origin of the x86 undefined instruction ud2 and clarifies why the mnemonic ends with the number 2. It uses this to illustrate broader quirks of x86 debugging conventions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中追溯了 x86 未定义指令 ud2 的起源，以及为何助记符中出现数字 2。用此揭示 x86 调试习惯中的一些小古怪。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/wp-content/uploads/sites/38/2019/02/ShowCover.jpg' data-full='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/wp-content/uploads/sites/38/2019/02/ShowCover.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Planet Labs 公開卫星数据供开发者访问，允许以编程方式获取影像与数据。这将推动研究、灾害应对和地理信息应用，但也提出许可和数据量方面的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>霍夫施塔特认为类比是人类认知与学习的核心，相关观点对 AI 设计和建模思维具有重要启示。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece examines how AI agents leverage test and verification techniques, highlighting current gaps, practical tooling, and evaluation metrics. It argues that reliable verification remains hard, requiring systematic approaches to ensure outputs are trustworthy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨 AI 代理在测试与验证技术方面的应用，指出现有工具与评估方法的不足，以及实现可验证性所面临的挑战。文章强调需要系统性的方法来提升输出的可信度与鲁棒性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/wordpress-automattic-ceo-matt-mullenweg-put-on-leave-of-absence/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Matt Mullenweg put on &#x27;leave of absence&#x27;</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49634650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 180</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49634650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Matt Mullenweg, cofounder of WordPress and head of Automattic, has taken a leave of absence, prompting questions about product roadmap and leadership continuity. The move could slow development or shift strategic focus as the company navigates competition with SaaS platforms and changes in the open-source ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WordPress共同创始人兼Automattic负责人 Matt Mullenweg 已进入休假状态，引发关于产品路线图和领导力连续性的讨论。此举可能减缓开发进度，或在与SaaS竞争与开源生态变动中改变战略定位。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>641A Room is a notorious telecom surveillance room; this entry reviews its early role in lawful interception and the privacy concerns it raised. It discusses architecture, policy context, and the ongoing debate over surveillance vs. civil liberties.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>641A 房间是知名的通讯监控设施，文章回顾其在合法截取中的早期作用及对隐私的影响。讨论相关架构、政策背景，以及监控与公民自由之间的持续博弈。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://snap.berkeley.edu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>λ Snap – An inviting programming language for kids and adults for CS study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Λ Snap：为儿童与成人设计的友好 CS 学习编程语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 180</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 112</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Λ Snap is pitched as a beginner-friendly language designed to teach CS concepts to both kids and adults. It emphasizes approachable syntax and incremental learning to bridge classroom theory and practical coding. For educators and self-learners, it could become a useful stepping stone toward more advanced languages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Λ Snap 被定位为一门面向初学者的编程语言，旨在帮助儿童与成人学习计算机科学概念。它强调易于理解的语法和渐进式学习， bridging 课堂理论与实际编码。对教师与自学者而言，可能成为迈向更高级语言的有用踏板。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/threat-intelligence-report-september-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Detecting and countering misuse of AI: September 2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>检测与应对 AI 滥用：2026 年 9 月</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49647300' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49647300' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 236</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The September 2026 threat intelligence report catalogs current methods for misusing AI, from prompt injection to data exfiltration, and outlines defender strategies. It emphasizes layered defenses, model monitoring, and red-teaming to stay ahead of attackers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>九月 2026 年威胁情报报告汇总了当前的 AI 滥用技术与防护对策，从提示注入到数据泄露，并提出分层防御、模型监控和红队演练等应对措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/7a4426f8ffe57e7de23ff36906fb1cc3efe2a82b-1200x630.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/7a4426f8ffe57e7de23ff36906fb1cc3efe2a82b-1200x630.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cordcuttersnews.com/tivo-plans-to-end-free-automatic-commercial-skipping-in-november-tests-paid-premium-replacement-service/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TiVo to charge money for skipping commercials in your own recordings</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TiVo 将就跳过广告收费</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49593971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49593971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TiVo plans to monetize ad-skipping in user-recordings, signaling a shift in DVR business model. The move could affect consumer expectations and how ad-skipping features are priced.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TiVo 将就用户录制节目中的广告跳过功能收费，体现了 DVR 商业模式的变化。此举可能影响用户预期以及对跳过广告功能的定价。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cordcuttersnews.com/wp-content/uploads/2016/09/Tivo-bolt1.jpeg' data-full='https://cordcuttersnews.com/wp-content/uploads/2016/09/Tivo-bolt1.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.johndcook.com/blog/2026/09/09/formal-method-revolution/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI’s Navier-Stokes release included a Lean 4 formal proof</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 的 Navier-Stokes 发布含 Lean 4 的形式化证明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49650326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49650326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 179</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI’s Navier-Stokes release includes a Lean 4 formal proof, highlighting a trend of merging formal methods with large language model research. The integration promises greater reliability and verifiability of AI systems, signaling a push toward rigour in AI tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 的 Navier-Stokes 发布中附带 Lean 4 的形式化证明，标志着将形式方法引入大型语言模型研究的趋势。此举有望提升AI系统的可靠性和可验证性，显示对更严格工程实践的推动。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.johndcook.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2022/05/twittercard.png' data-full='https://www.johndcook.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2022/05/twittercard.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://artificialworlds.net/blog/2026/09/11/feeling-sad-about-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Feeling Sad about AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对 AI 的悲观情绪</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49661506' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49661506' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 307</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece voices a tempered view on AI&#x27;s progress, critiquing hype and misaligned incentives. It advocates focusing on practical, human-centered AI developments and steady, responsible innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章对 AI 的发展表达了审慎悲观，批评市场炒作与目标错位。主张聚焦务实、以人为本的 AI 发展与稳健、负责任的创新路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://artificialworlds.net/blog/banner.png' data-full='https://artificialworlds.net/blog/banner.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49662767' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Privacy,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/kennethwolters/litelm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Litelm: LiteLLM Without the Bloat</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Litelm：去臃肿的轻量级 LLM 实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662767' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662767' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Litelm presents a compact LLM toolkit aimed at reducing dependencies and memory footprint for edge use. It targets speed and simplicity, enabling experiments with smaller models without heavyweight infrastructure. The project underscores a trend toward accessible, privacy-friendly AI tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Litelm 提供一个紧凑的 LLM 工具包，目标是降低依赖与内存占用，便于在边缘设备上使用。它强调速度与简洁性，支持在不依赖繁重基础设施的情况下尝试小型模型。该项目凸显向更易获取且注重隐私的 AI 工具的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/63940ca7f775953cc4b87515d7a6dca6c9580acafb3f47af77f92bf9852a13a7/kennethwolters/litelm' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/63940ca7f775953cc4b87515d7a6dca6c9580acafb3f47af77f92bf9852a13a7/kennethwolters/litelm' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49603546' data-tags='Show HN,Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Sadpainy/Stuxnet' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Stuxnet – A reconstructed source code of the infamous cyber-weapon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Stuxnet——臭名昭著网络武器的重建源码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49603546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 173</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49603546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A portion of Stuxnet&#x27;s source code has been reconstructed and published on GitHub, offering researchers a rare peek into the malware&#x27;s inner workings. The release illuminates the malware&#x27;s modular design, multi-stage propagation, and PLC targeting that made it historically sophisticated. For security teams, it serves as a concrete reference point for building defenses and understanding exploit chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>公开了一部分 Stuxnet 的重建源码，研究者可以通过该代码更深入理解这起臭名昭著的网络武器的实现。发布揭示了该木马的模块化设计、分阶段传播及对 PLC 的定向攻击等要点，为防御研究提供了参考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/497f54c571386e1be7253f0e53fecbf6618035358b5bf6efcd57c1a42b340343/Sadpainy/Stuxnet' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/497f54c571386e1be7253f0e53fecbf6618035358b5bf6efcd57c1a42b340343/Sadpainy/Stuxnet' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49679643' data-tags='AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hyperbo.la/w/aligned-to-whom/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Aligned to whom?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对齐对象究竟是谁？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49679643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49679643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 112</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Questions who AI alignment is really for: humans, corporations, regulators, or the broader ecosystem. The piece argues that alignment must consider multiple stakeholders and trade-offs, and that misidentifying the target can undermine safety goals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出 AI 对齐到底面向谁的问题：人类、企业、监管者还是整个生态系统。文章主张在多方利益与权衡之间寻找对齐目标，避免将对象设定错位而削弱安全目标。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hyperbo.la/social/w/aligned-to-whom.png' data-full='https://hyperbo.la/social/w/aligned-to-whom.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/09/06/science/92-year-old-mathematician-apprentice.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The 92-Year-Old Mathematician and the Teenage Apprentice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>92 岁数学家与青少年学徒</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A human-interest piece about intergenerational mentorship in mathematics, illustrating how experience and curiosity can fuel talent pipelines across generations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲述数学领域代际师徒关系的温情故事，展示经验与好奇心如何支撑跨代的人才培养。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49648732' data-tags='Business,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.inventbuild.studio/blog/genuine-creativity-is-your-new-moat' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Creativity is the new moat</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>创造力是新护城河</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49648732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49648732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Creativity is being positioned as the new competitive moat, as automation handles routine tasks while unique ideas, design, and storytelling differentiate products. The piece argues for investing in human creativity, culture, and originality as a durable advantage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>创造力被视为新型护城河，自动化处理日常任务，唯有独特的想象力、设计和叙事能力才能区分产品。文章建议投入人类创造力、文化与原创性，作为长期竞争优势。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://buttondown.com/blog/lotus-notes-email' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lotus Notes and the dangers of starting from scratch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lotus Notes：从零开始的风险</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49623937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49623937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 117</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Starting from scratch is seductive but risky; legacy systems like Lotus Notes illustrate the hidden costs of abandoning mature platforms. The piece argues for leveraging proven, well-integrated foundations rather than reinventing the wheel.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>从零开始的做法往往诱人却风险重重，像 Lotus Notes 这样的遗留系统案例揭示了放弃成熟平台所隐藏的成本与集成挑战。文章主张在更新中优先利用经过验证的、已高度集成的基础设施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://marketing.buttondown.com/og/generic?title=Lotus+Notes+and+the+dangers+of+starting+from+scratch&amp;subtitle=Matthew+Guay&amp;date=June+19%2C+2026&amp;category=just+for+fun' data-full='https://marketing.buttondown.com/og/generic?title=Lotus+Notes+and+the+dangers+of+starting+from+scratch&amp;subtitle=Matthew+Guay&amp;date=June+19%2C+2026&amp;category=just+for+fun' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49602582' data-tags='Business,Legal,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(201) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://electrek.co/2026/09/07/tesla-driver-assist-stop-sign-buena-vista/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Tesla ran a stop sign and killed a man, Full Self-Driving/Autopilot was on</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉闯停车标志致人死亡，自动驾驶/Autopilot 已开启</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49602582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49602582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 112</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A fatal crash involving a Tesla with Full Self-Driving/Autopilot enabled has reignited safety concerns around driver-assistance systems. The incident highlights regulatory scrutiny, the need for clear disclosures about system status, and the challenges of fault attribution in mixed-autonomy scenarios.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一起特斯拉在开启全自动驾驶/Autopilot 时闯停车标志致人死亡的事故，再度点燃对驾驶辅助系统的安全性关注。事件凸显监管审查、对系统状态透明度的需求，以及混合自动化场景下的责任认定挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2022/09/Tesla-Full-Self-Driving-Beta-10.69-barrier.jpg?quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;resize=1200,628' data-full='https://electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2022/09/Tesla-Full-Self-Driving-Beta-10.69-barrier.jpg?quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;resize=1200,628' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49640913' data-tags='Hardware,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techradar.com/health-fitness/smartwatches/thanks-to-siri-recaps-your-apple-watch-is-always-listening-as-you-go-about-your-day-but-apple-may-be-risking-a-meta-glasses-style-backlash' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Thanks to Siri Recaps, your Apple Watch is always listening</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Siri Recaps 让你的 Apple Watch 始终在监听</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49641401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49641401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Siri Recaps makes the Apple Watch surface more of your day, but it also implies ongoing listening and data handling that draws privacy scrutiny. The feature heightens concerns about ambient listening on wearables and could provoke regulatory or consumer backlash if not clearly communicated.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Siri Recaps 让 Apple Watch 能更全面地记录日常互动，但这也意味着设备持续监听和数据处理，引发隐私担忧。若未清楚说明并获得同意，可能招致监管或消费者的反弹。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bespoke proposes a programming language designed around courteous interaction and natural language cues, aiming to lower friction for learners and multilingual teams. It tests how social norms influence code-writing and tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bespoke 打算用面向礼貌互动和自然语言提示的编程语言，降低学习者和多语言团队的使用门槛，探索社会规范对编码习惯的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Interim Computer Museum curates short-lived exhibits of vintage computing hardware and software, offering a dynamic glimpse into tech history. By emphasizing temporary, community-driven displays, it demonstrates a flexible model for preserving artifacts when permanent institutions struggle for funding. It&#x27;s a reminder that hands-on history can thrive in small, adaptable spaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>临时计算机博物馆策展短期的计算机硬件和软件展览，提供对科技历史的动态观察。强调临时性与社区驱动的展示，展示在永久机构资金紧张时的灵活保存模式。提醒人们，亲身互动的历史也能在小型、灵活的空间里茁壮成长。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/09/12/revolut-confirms-customer-data-breach-through-fake-government-requests/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Revolut confirms customer data breach through fake government requests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Revolut 因伪装政府请求导致的客户数据泄露</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49682087' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 159</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49682087' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Revolut confirms a data breach resulting from attackers posing as government officials to obtain customer data. The incident highlights social engineering risks and the need for stronger verification and data minimization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Revolut 确认发生数据泄露，攻击者通过伪装成政府官员的请求获取部分客户信息。这一事件凸显社会工程攻击的风险，以及对数据最小化与加强身份验证的迫切需求。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49546964' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(212) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-09-trees-muscle-posture-newly-role.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tension wood: A &#x27;muscle&#x27; that can both bend and straighten plants</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>张力木：一种能弯曲也能拉直植物的“肌肉”</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49546964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49546964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tension wood is a specialized tissue in trees that generates contractile forces to adjust posture. Recent work reveals how this biological muscle-like mechanism enables bending and straightening, informing bio-inspired materials and forestry management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>张力木是一种能通过收缩力调节树体姿态的特殊组织。最新研究揭示了其类似肌肉的工作机制如何实现弯曲与拉直，为生物启发材料和林业管理带来启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/a-newly-discovered-rol.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/a-newly-discovered-rol.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49663054' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(213) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cia.gov/stories/story/cia-releases-presidents-daily-briefs-in-commemoration-of-the-25th-anniversary-of-9-11/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CIA Releases President&#x27;s Daily Briefs in Commemoration of 9/11</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CIA 为纪念9/11 发布总统每日简报</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49663054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49663054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In a commemorative move, the CIA released declassified President’s Daily Briefs from key years around 9/11. The material offers a window into how analysts framed threats and recommended actions under pressure, enhancing understanding of intelligence decision-making. The release contextualizes how intelligence shaping influenced policy choices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为纪念9/11，中央情报局公开了关键年份段落的总统每日简报解密材料。该材料揭示分析师在压力下如何界定威胁、提出行动建议，有助于理解情报决策过程。此次公开为理解情报如何影响政策选择提供了历史背景。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(214) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.netbsd.org/tnf/entry/netbsd_9_5_released_and' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NetBSD 9.5 released and EOL for NetBSD-9</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NetBSD 9.5 发布，NetBSD-9 进入终止支持</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49587636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 150</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49587636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NetBSD 9.5 arrives with security fixes and improvements, while NetBSD-9 reaches end-of-life. Users should plan migration to newer releases to stay supported and secure. The release underscores NetBSD&#x27;s ongoing commitment to portability and reliability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NetBSD 9.5 发布，NetBSD-9 系列进入停止维护阶段。用户应规划升级到更新版本以获得持续支持和安全性。此举彰显 NetBSD 在可移植性与稳定性方面的持续承诺。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(215) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ntsb.gov:443/news/press-releases/Pages/NR20260909.aspx' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NTSB issues investigative update on B-767 runway excursion accident in Miami</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NTSB 就迈阿密 B-767 跑道偏离事故发布调查更新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49650418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49650418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 281</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The NTSB issue updates on the B-767 runway excursion in Miami, detailing findings related to weather conditions, runway state, and crew actions. The release includes probable cause indicators and safety recommendations aimed at preventing similar incidents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>调查更新聚焦天气、跑道条件和机组操作等因素，给出可能原因线索与安全改进建议，帮助预防类似事故。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49662116' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(216) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://terrytao.wordpress.com/2026/09/11/a-severe-misalignment-of-ai-in-mathematics/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Misalignment of AI in Mathematics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数学中的 AI 错位</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tao argues that AI misalignment is especially stark in mathematics, where rigorous proofs require precise reasoning; current AI excels at pattern recognition but cannot reliably produce formal proofs. The essay calls for careful integration of AI as a tool with verification and human oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>陶哲轩指出，人工智能在数学领域的错位尤为明显；AI 擅长模式识别，但很难可靠地产生形式化证明。文中呼吁将 AI 作为可验证、需人类监督的工具谨慎使用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://secure.gravatar.com/blavatar/bd4bda4207561b6998f10dec44b570f04ff4072b20f89162d525b186dfca3e49?s=200&amp;ts=1789226191' data-full='https://secure.gravatar.com/blavatar/bd4bda4207561b6998f10dec44b570f04ff4072b20f89162d525b186dfca3e49?s=200&amp;ts=1789226191' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/09/11/matt-mullenweg-tells-automattic-staff-in-slack-hes-back-in-control-after-ceo-ouster/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Matt Mullenweg tells Automattic staff in Slack he&#x27;s back in control after ouster</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Matt Mullenweg 在 Slack 告诉 Automattic 员工，他在被罢黜后重新掌控局面</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49660104' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49660104' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Following an apparent ouster, Mullenweg tells Automattic staff via Slack that he’s back in control, signaling a renewed bid for direction at the company. The note hints at strategic changes and the persistence of founder-led governance in a distributed, WordPress-backed organization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在经历外部权力动荡后，Mullenweg 通过 Slack 向员工表态重新掌控 Automattic，暗示公司将迎来新方向。此举凸显创始人主导治理风格在分布式团队中的持续作用。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(219) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cbc.ca/news/canada/ontario-great-lakes-sturgeon-lifespan-study-9.7329250' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Great Lakes sturgeon may be 400 years old:Scientists rethinking how to save them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>五大湖鲟鱼可能寿命达400岁：科学家重新思考拯救策略</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49624456' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 147</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49624456' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New aging estimates suggest Great Lakes sturgeon may live up to 400 years, reshaping conservation priorities. If lifespans are that long, protecting mature individuals and preserving habitat and spawning grounds become crucial to recovery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新的年龄估计表明五大湖鲟鱼寿命或达400年，重新影响 conservation 策略。若寿命如此之长，保护成体、栖息地和繁殖场所成为关键，需调整资源配置。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49684196' data-tags='Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://paulgraham.com/powerful.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making Startups Powerful</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让初创企业变得更强大</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684196' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 143</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684196' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Paul Graham argues that startups should focus on building leverage and durable growth to become truly powerful, not just fast iterations. The piece offers practical guidance on focus, sequencing, and scaling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>保罗·格雷厄姆主张创业公司应追求可持续的影响力与杠杆效应，而不仅是短期扩张。文章给出创始人应关注的优先级、资源分配与扩张策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s.turbifycdn.com/aah/paulgraham/bel-7.gif' data-full='https://s.turbifycdn.com/aah/paulgraham/bel-7.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49667375' data-tags='Space,Science,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(221) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gadgetreview.com/starlinks-signal-leakage-is-threatening-radio-astronomys-most-critical-frequencies' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Starlink Signal Leakage Threatens Radio Astronomy&#x27;s Most Critical Frequencies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Starlink 信号泄漏威胁无线电天文学关键频段</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49667375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 143</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49667375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Starlink signal leakage threatens radio astronomy by contaminating protected frequency bands used for astronomical observations. The interference could degrade measurements from large surveys and impact future projects reliant on radio data. The issue highlights the challenge of satellite mega-constellations for scientific infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Starlink 的信号泄漏污染了用于天文观测的受保护频段，威胁无线电天文学的观测质量。干扰可能削弱大规模观测和未来项目对无线电数据的依赖。此问题凸显卫星巨星座对科学基础设施构成的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gadgetreview.com/wp-content/uploads/Starlinks-Signal-Leakage.jpg' data-full='https://www.gadgetreview.com/wp-content/uploads/Starlinks-Signal-Leakage.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49622561' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(222) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/codex-quantum-computing-experiments/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How GPT‑5.6 Sol helps run quantum computing experiments</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT‑5.6 Sol 如何帮助量子计算实验</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49622561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 143</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49622561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GPT-5.6 Sol assists quantum experiments by automating routine tasks and data handling, enabling researchers to iterate more quickly. While this accelerates experimentation, it also raises questions about automation&#x27;s boundaries in delicate lab work and the reproducibility of AI-assisted results. The piece frames this as a sign of AI moving from software to laboratory instrumentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GPT-5.6 Sol 在量子计算实验中通过自动化任务和数据处理来加速研究进程，同时也暴露了将语言模型用于敏感实验的局限与风险。作者将其视为 AI 从软件扩展到实验室仪器的一个信号。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/53pCremlimLBTRxuMEnPyB/41f2eb372bada1bc3b0f7b721e671c9a/SEO-Card.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/53pCremlimLBTRxuMEnPyB/41f2eb372bada1bc3b0f7b721e671c9a/SEO-Card.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(223) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vllm.ai/blog/2026-08-23-speculative-decoding-amd-gpus' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Speculative Decoding in vLLM on AMD GPUs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 AMD GPU 上的 vLLM 推测解码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49596054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49596054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Speculative decoding in vLLM on AMD GPUs demonstrates a technique to accelerate large-language-model inference. The work explores performance, cost efficiency, and wider accessibility of open-source LLM deployments beyond NVIDIA-dominated hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>vLLM 在 AMD 显卡上的推测解码演示了一种加速大语言模型推理的技术，展示了潜在的性能与成本效益，并推动开源大模型部署在非 NVIDIA 硬件上的可及性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://vllm.ai/og?title=Exploring%20Speculative%20Decoding%20in%20vLLM%20on%20AMD%20GPUs&amp;authors=AMD%20and%20Embedded%20LLM&amp;date=2026-08-23&amp;path=/blog' data-full='https://vllm.ai/og?title=Exploring%20Speculative%20Decoding%20in%20vLLM%20on%20AMD%20GPUs&amp;authors=AMD%20and%20Embedded%20LLM&amp;date=2026-08-23&amp;path=/blog' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(224) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://abio.substack.com/p/how-poor-people-buy-cars' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to buy a good car for $1000</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用1000美元买到好车的方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49667762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 139</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49667762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 209</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A guide to buying a reliable car on a tight budget, focusing on the realities of $1000 used-vehicle markets. It covers practical checks, trade-offs, and likely maintenance needs for budget buyers who rely on a car for essential mobility. The piece offers actionable tips for navigating scarce options without overpaying.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在预算极低的情况下，教你如何买到相对可靠的一辆二手车，聚焦于1000美元级市场的现实。文章列出实用的检查要点、取舍以及预算买家对维护成本的预期。为在有限选项中避免高价，提供了可操作的买车建议。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49677450' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(225) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/2026/9/12/pdoom/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>P(doom)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>P(doom)</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49677450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49677450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise meditation on existential risk metrics and how tiny probabilities can loom large for humanity&#x27;s future. The piece challenges readers to think about how to quantify and respond to low-probability, high-impact events.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对存在性风险的度量进行简短思考，指出极低概率事件也可能带来巨大影响，促使读者思考如何量化并应对高冲击、低概率的未来风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-09-12-pdoom-social.png' data-full='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-09-12-pdoom-social.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(226) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/i-fixed-a-tractor-john-deere-self-repair-service/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I fixed a tractor using John Deere&#x27;s self-repair service. Farmers aren&#x27;t sold</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我用 John Deere 的自修服务修好了拖拉机，农民还没买账</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49658672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 137</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49658672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 150</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author tried Deere&#x27;s self-repair service and found mixed results—some fixes were possible, but overall farmers remain skeptical due to parts, tools, and reliability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者尝试使用约翰迪尔自修服务，发现成效参差不齐，农民对该计划的信任度不高，原因包括所需工具、零件和可依赖性。文章凸显厂商掌控的生态系统与农民自主维修之间的张力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49561002' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(227) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://martypc.blogspot.com/2026/09/decoding-nec-v20-microcode.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Decoding the NEC V20 Microcode</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>解码 NEC V20 微代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49561002' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 136</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49561002' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reverse-engineering of the NEC V20&#x27;s microcode uncovers implementation details and subtle differences from Intel-compatible behavior, illuminating instruction decoding and microarchitectural quirks. Those findings help explain compatibility anomalies and are useful for preservation, emulation, and understanding historical CPU design trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 NEC V20 微代码的逆向分析揭示了实现细节以及与 Intel 兼容行为的细微差异，说明了指令解码和微架构上的特性。相关发现有助于解释兼容性异常，并对硬件保存、仿真以及理解历史 CPU 设计权衡具有参考价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEjskHCopDIB5dU1q0D0lGEI6lqkA6e9U01Bym_U9Bv1WKKfrLJtFfe33H2Y-Ewu_AKW7ToWmFMBLHQtvAzR1kbQWV-bOVJej0EPqefkGYf0LRRw8rzQbed490tfEjo85vWnxPhfffnec9fraZJku7ULbAj-jIB0gBksBozA8Jxhkc_L4a1zmB7dFp18mEgT/w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu/v20_die_shot_large_01.jpg' data-full='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEjskHCopDIB5dU1q0D0lGEI6lqkA6e9U01Bym_U9Bv1WKKfrLJtFfe33H2Y-Ewu_AKW7ToWmFMBLHQtvAzR1kbQWV-bOVJej0EPqefkGYf0LRRw8rzQbed490tfEjo85vWnxPhfffnec9fraZJku7ULbAj-jIB0gBksBozA8Jxhkc_L4a1zmB7dFp18mEgT/w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu/v20_die_shot_large_01.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49606281' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(228) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://emacsredux.com/blog/2026/09/02/working-with-git-worktrees-in-magit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Working with Git Worktrees in Magit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Magit 中使用 Git Worktree</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49606281' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 136</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49606281' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Magit now supports Git worktrees, allowing you to check out multiple branches in parallel from within Emacs. This reduces context switching and speeds up feature work, with commands to add, switch, and prune worktrees. It’s especially helpful for parallel explorations of related branches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Magit 现已在 Emacs 内部支持 Git worktree，允许你并行检出多个分支。这样可以减少上下文切换、加速功能开发，并提供添加、切换与清理工作树的命令。对于并行探索相关分支尤为有用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://emacsredux.com/assets/og-image.png' data-full='https://emacsredux.com/assets/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49595505' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(229) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theorangeduck.com/page/inverse-kinematics-foot-locking' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Inverse Kinematics and Foot Locking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>逆向运动学与脚部锁定</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49595505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 136</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49595505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains why foot locking or sliding happens in IK rigs, due to misaligned pole vectors, contact constraints, and animation sampling on uneven terrain. It outlines practical fixes—stiffer toe/ankle control, toe bones, better ground alignment, and constrained foot placement—to keep characters&#x27; feet planted convincingly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解释了在逆向运动学系统中脚部出现锁定或滑动的原因，常见原因包括支点向量不对齐、接触约束以及在不平地面上的采样问题。提出实际修正措施，如增加脚趾和踝部控制、改善脚部着地对齐，以及用约束保证脚部在地面上的稳定放置。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://theorangeduck.com/media/uploads/FootSliding/ik_setup.png' data-full='https://theorangeduck.com/media/uploads/FootSliding/ik_setup.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49668181' data-tags='Open Source,Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(230) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.minitap.ai/blog/i-expected-better-from-google' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google stole open source code without crediting the authors (Artemis/Minitap)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌在 Artemis/Minitap 项目中未对开源代码进行署名</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49668181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 136</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49668181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Allegations that Google used open-source Artemis/Minitap code without proper attribution raise questions about license compliance and corporate accountability in software. The dispute underscores the importance of attribution in OSS and could strain relations with the open-source community. The case adds to broader debates about governance in industrial AI and cloud platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指称谷歌在 Artemis/Minitap 项目中未对开源代码进行署名，质疑其版权合规与企业问责。此事凸显开源署名的重要性，可能影响与开源社区的关系。事件成为关于工业 AI 与云平台治理的更广泛辩论的一部分。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/5b5hto6c/production/8a4a9cd2ceb3a1679ca5033e6209fc7d558e81a0-1360x980.png' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/5b5hto6c/production/8a4a9cd2ceb3a1679ca5033e6209fc7d558e81a0-1360x980.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49653328' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(231) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cell.com/cell-reports-medicine/fulltext/S2666-3791(26)00405-2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Exercise intensity is associated with cardiometabolic health</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>运动强度与心代谢健康相关</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49653328' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 135</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49653328' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Strong association found between exercise intensity and cardiometabolic health markers; higher-intensity workouts may yield greater benefits beyond total volume, but require balancing safety. The study informs exercise prescriptions for better metabolic outcomes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现运动强度与心代谢健康指标存在显著相关性；高强度锻炼在总量相同的情况下可能带来更大健康收益，但需兼顾安全性。研究结果为制定更有效的运动处方提供依据。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(232) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://electrek.co/2026/09/01/tesla-solar-roof-exit-installers-losses/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla killing Solar Roof is leaving installers with six-figure losses</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉太阳能屋顶计划退出致安装商损失六位数</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49601846' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 135</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49601846' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 123</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tesla’s Solar Roof program is exiting, leaving installers with six-figure losses and forcing a shift in the solar roofing market. The move illustrates the tension between product strategy, market demand, and the economics of specialized solar solutions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特斯拉太阳能屋顶计划退出，导致安装商遭遇六位数损失，并推动太阳能屋顶市场的结构性调整。这一举措揭示了产品策略、市场需求与专业化太阳能解决方案经济性之间的博弈。</p>
