<p class='hn-title'>(234) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kepter.app/finder' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Finder is so frustrating and has been since day one</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Finder 从一开始就让人很挫败</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49589722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 134</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49589722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 197</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique of Finder, macOS&#x27;s file manager, highlighting long-standing UX issues, missing features, and the mismatch between user expectations and how Finder handles files. The piece advocates for alternative file managers or improved design from Apple.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章批评 macOS 的 Finder 长期存在的用户体验问题、功能缺失，以及与用户期望的不一致。文中主张考虑替代的文件管理器或提出对 Apple 做出重大设计改进的呼声。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49600997' data-tags='Legal,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(235) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.usatoday.com/story/news/nation/2026/09/07/court-constitution-right-clean-water/91649488007/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>No constitutional right to clean water, federal court finds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>联邦法院认定没有宪法层面的清洁水权利</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49600997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49600997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 167</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A federal court has ruled there is no explicit constitutional right to clean water, a decision with broad implications for environmental policy and litigation. The ruling may influence how communities pursue infrastructure funding and protections for water access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>联邦法院裁定宪法未明确保障清洁用水权利，这一判决对环境政策与诉讼路径具有广泛影响，可能影响社区在水资源基础设施资助与保护方面的行动空间。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(236) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pointersgonewild.com/2026-08-25-replacing-a-rust-enum-with-a-64-bit-word/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Replacing a Rust Enum with a 64-Bit Word Made My Interpreter 17% Faster</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 64 位字替換 Rust 枚舉讓解釋器快 17%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49575914' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49575914' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Replacing a Rust enum with a 64-bit word boosted an interpreter’s speed by 17%, illustrating how representation choices impact performance. The gain comes with trade-offs in readability and safety, reminding implementers to weigh low-level optimizations against maintainability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>將 Rust 枚舉替換為 64 位字使解釈器性能提升約 17%，展示了表示方式對性能的影響。此類提升伴隨可讀性與安全性的取捨，提醒實作者在考量維護性時再評估低層優化。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(237) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.latimes.com/california/story/2026-09-12/juveniles-riding-in-waymo-arrested-after-police-find-ghost-gun' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Waymo pulls over, calls cops on juvenile riders who had &#x27;ghost gun&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Waymo拦下未成年乘客并报警，车内发现幽灵枪</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49672549' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49672549' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 215</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Waymo reportedly stopped a ride and called police after juveniles in the car were found to possess a ghost gun. The incident underscores safety and liability concerns around weapons in autonomous-vehicle contexts and how law enforcement responds to riders. It raises questions about weapon safety, youth encounters, and corporate-police interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Waymo 在车内发现未成年人携带幽灵枪后报警并停车。此事件凸显自动驾驶场景下的武器安全与责任问题，以及执法机关对乘客的处置方式。涉及武器安全、未成年乘客以及企业与警方的互动等议题。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49668777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49668777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new Usenet rewind archive search engine launches, enabling researchers and nostalgic users to search historical Usenet postings. The project highlights ongoing interest in long-term digital preservation and accessible archival search. It offers a window into decades of online discourse that would otherwise be hard to explore.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 Usenet 倒带档案搜索引擎上线，方便研究者和怀旧用户检索历史 Usenet 帖子。该项目显示对长期数字保存和可检索档案的持续兴趣。它为研究几十年的在线话语提供了新的入口。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(240) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://merybenavente.me/blog/proof-of-capture' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Proof of Capture: Apple Reference Image, but open source and using steganography</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Proof of Capture：基于苹果参考图像的开源隐写证明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49649222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49649222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project proposes Proof of Capture using an Apple reference image, implemented openly and leveraging steganography to prove a photo&#x27;s provenance and capture time. This provides a lightweight approach to image provenance and tamper resistance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该方案以苹果参考图像为核心，开源实现并通过隐写技术标记信息，帮助验证图片的来源与拍摄时间。这为数字图像的溯源与抗篡改提供了一种轻量办法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://merybenavente.me/blog/proof-of-capture/camera-enclosure.png' data-full='https://merybenavente.me/blog/proof-of-capture/camera-enclosure.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(241) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Speedstu/CUDA-for-AMD-Windows' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CUDA for AMD on Windows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows 上的 AMD CUDA 实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A community project brings CUDA-like tooling to AMD GPUs on Windows, enabling CUDA-dependent workflows to run on non-NVIDIA hardware. While not a drop-in replacement, it broadens hardware choices for ML developers and hobbyists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>社区项目让 AMD 显卡在 Windows 上获得类似 CUDA 的体验，扩展了不能使用 NVIDIA 的硬件场景。尽管不是完全替代，但为 ML 开发者和爱好者带来更广的硬件选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/22d1e13ab927208699e46c73f93970f2cdbd96a5a5c6a24711458d4508c4bda5/Speedstu/CUDA-for-AMD-Windows' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/22d1e13ab927208699e46c73f93970f2cdbd96a5a5c6a24711458d4508c4bda5/Speedstu/CUDA-for-AMD-Windows' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(242) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.co.uk/news/articles/crm932el3yjo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We&#x27;ve followed their lives for six decades; now the stars of 7 Up are bowing out</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>六十年如一日：7 Up 之星如今将退出</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49670981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49670981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Six decades into the Up series, the stars are bowing out as new installments conclude, marking a turning point in a landmark documentary about social class, choice, and life trajectories. The departure signals both the aging of the subjects and a shift in how longitudinal human-study media is produced and consumed. The films have shaped public perception of social mobility and storytelling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在深入拍摄六十年之久的《7 Up》系列中，参与者如今逐渐退出，标志着这部关于社会阶层、选择与人生轨迹的里程碑式纪录片走向终章。此举既反映主演者的衰老，也凸显纵向人类研究题材在制作与传播上的变迁。该系列对公众对社会流动性和叙事方式的认知产生了深远影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_news/1200/cpsprodpb/e7de/live/4f95a3f0-a782-11f1-8858-e93489ec40ef.png' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_news/1200/cpsprodpb/e7de/live/4f95a3f0-a782-11f1-8858-e93489ec40ef.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>实用的 Linux 建议：交换文件比分区更灵活、管理更简便，包含创建、启用与调优交换文件以提升性能的步骤。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(246) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/world/2026/aug/28/never-tidy-paramedics-shoes-japan-custom-etiquette' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Please don&#x27;t rearrange our shoes when we turn up, paramedics in Japan urge</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本急救人员请勿打理我们的鞋子</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本的急救人员呼吁旁观者在现场避免整理或移动病人鞋子与随身物品，强调尊重现场流程与安全注意事项。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(247) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Larger_Pacific_striped_octopus' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Larger Pacific striped octopus</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型太平洋条纹章鱼</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49621018' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49621018' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Larger Pacific Striped Octopus is a notable Pacific coast species known for its striped coloration and relatively larger size among octopuses. It underscores how marine biodiversity still contains surprising and little‑known species that pique scientific interest.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>更大号的太平洋条纹章鱼是太平洋海岸的一个显著物种，因其条纹颜色和相对较大的体型而引人注目。它凸显海洋生物多样性中仍存在一些鲜为人知、值得科研关注的物种。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chorleywood 面包工艺革新了英国的面包生产，实现了大规模的快速、低成本生产，同时在口感与质感上存在取舍。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(251) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.righto.com/2026/09/8087-microcode-reverse-engineering-fscale.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microcode in Intel&#x27;s 8087 floating-point chip: the scale instruction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英特尔8087微代码：scale指令</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49673580' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49673580' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The 8087 floating-point unit uses microcode to implement the scale instruction, revealing early FPUs’ approach to performing arithmetic scaling. The reverse-engineering sheds light on design trade-offs and the performance quirks of historic hardware. A small detail with a surprising window into compiler and CPU evolution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>8087浮点单元通过微代码实现scale指令，暴露了早期FPU在执行浮点缩放时的实现思路与权衡。对这段历史硬件的逆向分析揭示了设计取舍和性能特征，让人一窥编译器与CPU演进的细节。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/blogger_img_proxy/AEn0k_uU8Z4c5i0F_KCKCrMfGdcXyNE_aVwIGn3fXFnBncScvuTKGvcuM5G07IkzP4uaORnWhYUG0C0l7cmGvKyiuftyT2i8txjOb-BtnAsgoaCcLArJwv075BdvPRfMe-L3BkWr-MD3dvqv-lQ=w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu' data-full='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/blogger_img_proxy/AEn0k_uU8Z4c5i0F_KCKCrMfGdcXyNE_aVwIGn3fXFnBncScvuTKGvcuM5G07IkzP4uaORnWhYUG0C0l7cmGvKyiuftyT2i8txjOb-BtnAsgoaCcLArJwv075BdvPRfMe-L3BkWr-MD3dvqv-lQ=w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sprinklz.io/public/pdwt4dve5uai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hacker News with reduced priority for AI driven content</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hacker News降低 AI 驱动内容的优先级</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49660482' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49660482' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hacker News reduces priority for AI-generated content, affecting how AI-focused posts surface in the feed. The change could influence community engagement and the visibility of AI research discussions. It adds to ongoing debates about algorithmic governance and authenticity in user-generated platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hacker News 将 AI 驱动内容的优先级降低，影响相关帖文在信息流中的曝光。此变动可能改变社区参与度及 AI 研究讨论的可见性。引发关于算法治理与用户生成平台真实性的持续讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49665711' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(253) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dwarkesh.com/p/john-beren-charlie' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI researchers debate how close we are to recursive self-improvement</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 研究者辩论我们离递归自我改进有多近</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49665711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 118</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49665711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI researchers debate how close we are to recursive self-improvement, weighing architectural breakthroughs against self-modifying systems. The discussion touches on safety, governance, and the pace of future capabilities, shaping how funding and policy respond to rapid progress. The takeaway: consensus remains elusive, but consensus-building itself matters for risk management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 研究者就递归自我改进的距离进行辩论，权衡架构突破与自我修改系统的潜力。讨论涉及安全、治理以及未来能力的增速，影响对快速进展的投资与政策走向。共识尚未形成，但建立共识对风险管理也很关键。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!qkOD!,w_1200,h_600,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-video.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fvideo_upload%2Fpost%2F215199144%2F127b288b-15be-4ae5-b75f-8148f6a3ca45%2Ftranscoded-1789142822.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!qkOD!,w_1200,h_600,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-video.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fvideo_upload%2Fpost%2F215199144%2F127b288b-15be-4ae5-b75f-8148f6a3ca45%2Ftranscoded-1789142822.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(254) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Will there be a 7G?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>会不会有第七代通信技术？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49674498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49674498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 172</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thoughtful look at the prospects for a hypothetical 7G wireless technology, examining fundamental limits, potential architectures, and the long horizon for new generations. The paper argues breakthroughs will rely on novel physics and design paradigms rather than incremental improvements, placing 7G in a long telecom evolution timeline.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文从物理极限与技术路径出发，探讨假想的第七代通信（7G）的前景。作者认为需要突破性材料与全新架构等创新，而非简单的迭代式改进，并将代际跃迁置于更长的电信发展时间线中。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49671159' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(256) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tedium.co/2026/09/11/ilands-agents-email-spam-kaixin-tang/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The worst spam emails: iLands AI agent hustle</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>最糟糕的垃圾邮件：iLands AI 代理骗局</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49671159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49671159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An overview of spam campaigns using AI agents branded as iLands to scale social engineering, showing how automation raises the scale and realism of scams. The piece cautions readers to watch for agent-like behaviors and to verify requests carefully.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍了利用 AI 代理进行规模化社交工程的垃圾邮件骗局，代理人被标记为 iLands，提升了骗术的逼真度与规模。提醒读者警惕基于代理的欺诈行为，并对请求进行严格核验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://v1.screenshot.11ty.dev/https%3A%2F%2Ftedium.co%2Fog-images%2F2026%2F09%2F11%2Filands-agents-email-spam-kaixin-tang%2F/opengraph/?v=1789176752' data-full='https://v1.screenshot.11ty.dev/https%3A%2F%2Ftedium.co%2Fog-images%2F2026%2F09%2F11%2Filands-agents-email-spam-kaixin-tang%2F/opengraph/?v=1789176752' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>渲染中的拓扑绘本</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A rendered topological picture book that uses visuals to convey ideas like connectedness and holes. It showcases how data-driven art and math visualization can make abstract topology accessible and engaging.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>渲染成册的拓扑绘本通过直观图片展示连通性、孔洞等拓扑概念。该作品将数据驱动的艺术与拓扑可视化结合，使抽象的拓扑更易理解、富有吸引力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(258) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://system76.com/workstations/thelio-mira-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Thelio Mira AI Linux Workstation: 192 GB GPU Memory</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Thelio Mira AI Linux Workstation ships with 192 GB of GPU memory, signaling a serious workstation option for on‑premise AI workloads. It showcases the growing demand for high‑end GPUs in a Linux‑centric workstation ecosystem and could affect how teams prototype models locally.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Thelio Mira AI Linux 工作站配备 192 GB 显存，成为本地 AI 工作负载的强劲选择。该机型凸显了对高端 GPU 的需求在 Linux 工作站生态中的增长，可能改变团队在本地原型测试的方式。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(259) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ras.ac.uk/news-and-press/research-highlights/fingerprints-inside-sun-could-reveal-if-it-once-swallowed-planet' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Fingerprints&#x27; inside the Sun could reveal if it once swallowed a planet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>太阳内部的指纹可能揭示它是否曾经吞噬过一颗行星</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49683033' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 109</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49683033' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astronomers detect signatures inside the Sun that could record a past planetary ingestion, offering a novel way to probe solar interior dynamics and planetary formation history.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>天文学家在太阳内层发现“指纹”式信号，可能记录了曾经吞噬行星的历史。该发现为研究太阳内部结构和行星形成史提供新线索。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(260) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Harnessing the Universal Geometry of Embeddings</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>利用嵌入的普遍几何结构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49590595' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49590595' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper argues for a universal geometry of embeddings, providing a common lens to compare semantic spaces across models. It outlines how geometric structure can inform alignment, transfer learning, and evaluation of embeddings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文提出将嵌入向量的几何结构放在统一框架下理解，帮助比较不同模型中的语义空间。该视角有助于改进对齐、迁移学习与嵌入评估的方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 LaTeX 排版的复古科学论文</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49629624' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49629624' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A collection of vintage scientific papers typeset in LaTeX, preserving classic scholarship with modern typography. It showcases how LaTeX can recreate historical layouts and improve readability while maintaining archival value.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个用 LaTeX 排版的复古科学论文合集，既保留经典学术风格，又提升可读性，展示了现代排版对历史文献的再现能力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49687312' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(262) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=koclOnlde0E' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flock cameras used to arrest a child for playing on a swing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flock 摄像头被用来因孩子在荡秋千而逮捕孩子</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49687312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49687312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A case shows Flock cameras and facial recognition were used to arrest a child merely for playing on a swing, underscoring serious concerns about bias, civil liberties, and misidentification in surveillance tech. It highlights the risks of relying on automated policing for everyday behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一宗案件显示，Flock 摄像头与人脸识别被用于仅因孩子在荡秋千而逮捕其人，暴露监控技术在偏见、公民自由与误识别方面的严重风险。事件凸显了将自动化执法用于日常行为的隐忧。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49540827' data-tags='Design,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(263) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://worksthatwork.com/6/unified-arabic' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Unified Arabic</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>统一阿拉伯文</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49540827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49540827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The project proposes a unified approach to Arabic typography or orthography aimed at improving legibility and consistency across digital platforms. By reconciling regional variants and layout challenges, it seeks to make Arabic rendering more reliable for designers and engineers working with multilingual interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目提出了一套旨在提升数字平台上阿拉伯文可读性与一致性的统一排版或正字法方案。通过调和区域变体与版面难题，目标是让阿拉伯文在多语言界面中的呈现对设计师和工程师来说更可靠、更易处理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://worksthatwork.com/6/assets/Articles/91/Images/patent-beiruti2.jpg' data-full='https://worksthatwork.com/6/assets/Articles/91/Images/patent-beiruti2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49643543' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(264) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/gauravapiscean/agentic-kv-cache' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LRU is harder to beat than the KV-cache papers suggest</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LRU比KV缓存论文所示的更难被打败</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49643543' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49643543' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New measurements suggest that LRU remains a robust baseline under realistic workloads, harder to outperform than some KV-cache proposals imply. The finding has practical implications for how systems architects choose cache strategies and benchmark performance. It reinforces the value of well-understood, simple policies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新实验表明，在真实工作负载下，最近提出的KV缓存方案并不总能超越LRU，LRU的表现反而更稳健。此发现影响系统架构在缓存策略与性能基准上的取舍，强化了简单、易于理解的策略的价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6388e91e906a7ac309e067428fe3ea062daf96f4c46d6b62ae3e4d7275f3db78/gauravapiscean/agentic-kv-cache' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6388e91e906a7ac309e067428fe3ea062daf96f4c46d6b62ae3e4d7275f3db78/gauravapiscean/agentic-kv-cache' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49605822' data-tags='AI,Finance,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(265) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/TauricResearch/TradingAgents' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Multi-Agents LLM Financial Trading Framework</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>多代理LLM金融交易框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49605822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49605822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A modular framework enables multiple LLM agents to coordinate trading decisions, potentially expanding automated strategies beyond single-model limits. It unlocks speed and diversification but also raises risk management, reproducibility, and regulatory considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个模块化框架允许多名LLM代理协同进行交易决策，理论上扩展了自动化策略的边界。它带来更高的速度与策略多样性，同时也带来风险管理、可重复性与监管方面的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/909213664/8cfc671d-b54b-400e-beab-8ef0bbf39aa1' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/909213664/8cfc671d-b54b-400e-beab-8ef0bbf39aa1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(266) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blinkenlights.de/en/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Project Blinkenlights</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Blinkenlights 计划：把建筑变成巨型显示屏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49666146' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49666146' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Project Blinkenlights documents a public art/engineering project that turns a building into a giant LED display driven by software and hardware. It illustrates how creative engineering can fuse hardware, software, and public space to create immersive, real-time visuals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Project Blinkenlights 记录了一个将建筑变成巨型 LED 显示屏的公共艺术与工程项目，由软件与硬件驱动。该计划展示了创意工程如何在公共空间中将硬件、软件与实时可视化融合，创造沉浸式体验。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49629209' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(267) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.not-ship.com/united-nations-map/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The UN challenges five centuries of cartography</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>联合国挑战五个世纪的制图学</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49629209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49629209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 209</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The UN is rethinking traditional cartography using modern geospatial tools, open data, and inclusive mapping practices to modernize global maps. Expect new standards and more accessible, accurate geospatial data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>联合国正以现代制图工具、开放数据和包容性制图实践重新审视传统地图，旨在更新全球地图并提升地理数据的可用性与准确性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(268) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://claytonwramsey.com/blog/seeing-eye/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m a seeing-eye dog for a computer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我是电脑的导盲犬</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49550276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49550276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 88</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal essay frames humans as a seeing-eye companion for computers, underscoring human-in-the-loop practices to keep AI outputs accurate and trustworthy. It shares practical habits for guiding systems through ambiguity and error.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者把自己比作电脑的导盲犬，强调在人机协作中人类的引导作用，帮助 AI 输出更准确、可信。文中给出在不确定情境中引导系统的实用方法与习惯。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://claytonwramsey.com/blog/seeing-eye/lid1.png' data-full='https://claytonwramsey.com/blog/seeing-eye/lid1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49589190' data-tags='Data,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(269) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.vldb.org/pvldb/volumes/19/paper/The%20Dataflow%20Model%20Revisited' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Dataflow Model Revisited</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>重访数据流模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49589190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49589190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper revisits the dataflow computation model to clarify operator semantics, state handling, and composability for modern streaming and batch systems. By formalizing dataflow primitives and execution expectations, it aims to help system builders reason about correctness, latency, and fault recovery across distributed data-processing engines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该论文对数据流计算模型进行了重新梳理，阐明了算子语义、状态处理和可组合性，以适应现代流式与批处理系统的需求。通过形式化数据流的基本原语和执行预期，旨在帮助系统设计者在分布式数据处理引擎中更好地推理正确性、延迟和容错恢复。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(270) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49596976' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apparently CodePen 2.0 sends data to their servers as you type</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CodePen 2.0 在你输入时将数据发送到服务器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49596976' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49596976' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Users noted that CodePen 2.0 may send keystrokes to servers as you type, raising privacy concerns about editor telemetry. The disclosure invites scrutiny of data collection, and may push developers toward more transparent privacy controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有用户指出 CodePen 2.0 在打字时会将按键数据发送到服务器，引发对编辑器遥测的隐私担忧。此事促使人们审视数据收集透明度，并推动开发者改进隐私控制。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(272) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://geo-tp.github.io/ESP32-Bit-Pirate/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ESP32 Bit Pirate Hardware Hacking Kit with Web Tools That Speaks Every Protocol</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A compact ESP32-based hardware-hacking kit featuring web tools that probe, emulate, and analyze a wide range of protocols. It lowers barriers for hobbyists and researchers to experiment with UART, I2C, CAN, Wi-Fi, BLE, and more.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(274) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lemire.me/blog/2026/09/03/python-sets-and-dictionaries-can-have-quadratic-time-performance/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Python sets and dictionaries can have quadratic-time performance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Python 的集合和字典在最坏情况下可能达到二次方级别的性能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49612470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49612470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers show that Python sets and dicts can experience quadratic-time performance under adversarial input or collisions, impacting worst-case algorithm guarantees.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者指出在对抗性输入或哈希冲突下，Python 的集合与字典可能出现二次时间复杂度的 Worst-Case 行为，影响最坏情况的算法性能。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(276) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://transformer-circuits.pub/2021/framework/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Mathematical Framework for Transformer Circuits (2021)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>变换器电路的数学框架（2021）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49672365' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49672365' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper lays out a precise mathematical framework for transformer circuits, clarifying how information travels through attention and feed-forward blocks. It provides a lens for interpreting model internals, highlights bottlenecks, and could guide more efficient or interpretable designs. These ideas matter for researchers aiming to improve reliability and efficiency of large language models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该框架为变换器电路的工作机制提供清晰的数学描述，揭示信息在注意力及前馈模块中的流动路径。它为解读模型内部运作提供新视角，指出瓶颈并有望推动更高效、可解释的架构设计。对追求提升大模型可靠性与效率的研究者具有重要意义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Romania&#x27;s soccer authorities introduce a &#x27;black card&#x27; to curb abusive behavior by parents on the sidelines, aiming to protect referees and players. The move signals growing intolerance for sideline harassment and reflects evolving governance of youth sports.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>罗马尼亚足球机构引入‘黑牌’，以遏制场边家长的辱骂行为，保护裁判和球员。此举体现对场边骚扰的零容忍态度，并反映出青少年体育治理正在演进。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(280) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/alphagenome-atlas' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AlphaGenome maps 9B DNA variants</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AlphaGenome 绘制九十亿 DNA 变异图谱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49624717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49624717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A large-scale genome atlas catalogs nine billion DNA variants, providing a richer map of human genetic diversity. The resource could accelerate disease association studies, population genetics, and precision medicine, but also raises questions about data sharing, privacy, and equitable access to genomic insights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个大规模的基因组图谱揭示九十亿个 DNA 变异，极大丰富了人类遗传多样性的图景。该资源可能加速疾病关联研究、群体遗传学和精准医疗，但也引发关于数据共享、隐私与公平获取的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67719885&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C125%2C0%2C125' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67719885&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C125%2C0%2C125' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(281) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://00f.net/2026/06/23/webassembly-runtimes-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Performance of WebAssembly Runtimes in 2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2026年WebAssembly运行时的性能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49623933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 97</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49623933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The 2026 landscape shows wasm runtimes maturing with improvements in speed, memory usage, and startup latency. These gains expand the viability of wasm for edge and serverless workloads, and influence language runtime design and deployment choices for developers. It signals wasm becoming a more practical standard for portable code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2026年的WebAssembly运行时已趋于成熟，速度、内存使用与启动延迟等方面均有提升。这些改进扩大了WebAssembly在边缘计算和无服务器工作负载中的应用场景，并影响开发者在运行时设计与部署的选择，标志着 wasm 成为更实用的可移植代码标准。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49652028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49652028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>YuE2 explores using symbolic planning to guide frontier music generation, combining structured AI planning with creative composition. The project illustrates a novel direction for controllable AI music and might inform future research on hybrid symbolic‑statistical approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>YuE2 将符号规划用于引导前沿音乐创作，将结构化的 AI 规划与创作结合起来，展示了可控 AI 音乐的一个新方向。该项目可能为混合符号‑统计方法的未来研究提供线索。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(283) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ptree.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Interactive Tree of Life</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：互动式生命之树</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49559069' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49559069' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Interactive Tree of Life presents an interactive visualization of biological taxonomy, enabling users to explore relationships from broad groups to species. The project demonstrates how scalable frontend tech can render vast hierarchical data with search, filtering, and navigation tools. It&#x27;s a useful example for biology education and data visualization on the web.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Interactive Tree of Life 提供一个可交互的生命之树可视化，允许用户浏览从大类到物种的系统发育关系并进行搜索与过滤。该项目展示了大规模分级数据在前端的可视化实现，以及在教育和研究中的潜在应用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(284) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openjdk.org/jeps/544' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>JEP 544: Ahead-of-Time Code Compilation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>JEP 544：Ahead-of-Time 代码编译</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49647404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49647404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>JEP 544 outlines bringing ahead-of-time compilation to the Java ecosystem, aiming for faster startup and lower memory footprints. The proposal weighs benefits against JVM&#x27;s dynamic optimization model, toolchain integration, and cross-language interoperability, with implications for GraalVM and native-image strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>JEP 544 提出将 Ahead-of-Time 编译引入 Java 生态，目标是更快的启动时间和更低的峰值内存。需要权衡动态优化、工具链整合以及与原生镜像的互操作性，对 GraalVM 等生态有潜在影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(288) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/marsbos/mador' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Mador – Make any DOM reactive with a tiny 80-line Proxy state tuple</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Mador——用 80 行 Proxy 状态元组让任意 DOM 实现响应式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49590738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 93</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49590738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mador is a tiny library that makes any DOM reactive using an 80-line Proxy-based state tuple. It demonstrates that practical reactivity can be achieved with a tiny footprint, offering a minimal entry point into how reactive systems work. A useful reference for experimenting with lean, framework-free UI design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mador 是一个极简库，使用 80 行 Proxy 状态元组即可让任意 DOM 具备响应式能力。它展示了以极小的体积实现响应式的可能性，为理解响应式编程提供了简练的入门范例。对于探索无框架、精简 UI 设计的开发者颇具参考价值。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49684101' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(291) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unsung.aresluna.org/key-symbols-we-lost-to-time-pt-1-the-pc-side/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Key symbols we lost to time, pt. 1: The PC side</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>时光遗失的按键符号，第一部分：PC 端</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684101' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 92</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684101' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores historical keyboard symbols that faded from modern PCs, tracing their origins and why some glyphs disappeared over time.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>第一部分回顾 PC 时代的关键符号，探讨哪些符号逐渐淡出主流键盘，以及背后的历史原因。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A manifesto-style take on version control that argues current tools strain under modern workflows and proposes a next-generation system. It envisions new architectures, possibly CRDT-based, to improve collaboration, history management, and performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出对版本控制的新愿景，认为现有工具在现代工作流中存在局限，探讨更具可扩展性与易用性的下一代版本控制方案，可能包含 CRDT 等新架构以提升协作与性能。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A US Customs supervisor was busted for stealing Core i7 CPUs, RAM, and hard drives from Homeland Security PCs, swapping in inferior hardware and cashing out on Newegg.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名美国海关主管因从国土安全部电脑窃取硬件并以低配件替换后变现而被逮捕，凸显了机构级硬件安全与审计的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49658819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49658819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A surgeon notes that exposures from 9/11 continue to drive cancer risk for many patients, underscoring long-tail health effects for first responders and residents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位纽约胸外科医生指出，9/11 的暴露仍在推动许多患者的癌症风险，凸显对一线 responder 与居民的长期健康影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49644179' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(298) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://terriblesoftware.org/2026/09/10/ai-is-breaking-this-thing-we-call-trust/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Is Breaking This Thing We Call Trust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 正在侵蚀我们所称的信任</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49644179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49644179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As AI-generated content and deepfakes erode authenticity, trust becomes a design constraint for AI systems, requiring provenance, watermarking, and risk-aware UX. It argues for robust governance and user education.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 生成内容和深度伪造正在侵蚀真实性，信任成为 AI 系统设计的约束。需要可追溯性、水印、以及面向风险的用户体验，并加强治理与用户教育。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/terriblesoftware.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/cHJpdmF0ZS9sci9pbWFnZXMvd2Vic2l0ZS8yMDIyLTA1L2lzMTkzODMtaW1hZ2Uta3d2d3MwemwuanBn.webp?fit=1024%2C683&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/terriblesoftware.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/cHJpdmF0ZS9sci9pbWFnZXMvd2Vic2l0ZS8yMDIyLTA1L2lzMTkzODMtaW1hZ2Uta3d2d3MwemwuanBn.webp?fit=1024%2C683&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(299) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/information-technology/2026/09/vmware-migration-reduces-tottenham-hotspurs-licensing-fees-by-85-percent/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>VMware migration reduces Tottenham Hotspur&#x27;s licensing fees by 85 percent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>VMware 迁移让托特纳姆热刺的许可费下降 85%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49596797' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49596797' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tottenham Hotspur&#x27;s VMware migration slashes licensing fees by 85%, illustrating the cost benefits of virtualization and enterprise software alignment. The case may influence other organizations weighing vendor contracts and virtualization strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>托特纳姆热刺完成 VMware 迁移，许可费用下降约 85%，体现了虚拟化和软件合规带来的成本收益。此案例可能影响其他机构在供应商合约和虚拟化策略上的决策。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(300) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://publicdomainreview.org/collection/john-margolies-photographs-of-roadside-america/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>John Margolies&#x27; photographs of roadside America</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>约翰·马格洛利斯的路边美国摄影作品</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49560682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49560682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>John Margolies’ roadside America photographs capture a bygone era of American road culture and signage, now archived and celebrated for design history and cultural memory. The collection informs interface design, travel UI, and the aesthetics of mid-20th-century commerce.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>约翰·马格洛利斯的路边美国摄影记录了过去的美式公路文化、路牌与景观，如今被整理成珍贵的设计史与文化记忆。该收藏为界面设计、旅行相关 UI 以及20世纪中期商业美学提供素材。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49660676' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,Open Source,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(301) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/godot-pty/gpty' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Godot and Rust based multiplexer (terminal panes and more)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于 Godot 与 Rust 的终端分屏多路复用器（pane 等功能）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49660676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49660676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open-source terminal multiplexer built with Godot for UI and Rust for the backend PTY multiplexing. It aims to deliver modern panes and a smoother UX beyond traditional tmux-like tools, potentially reshaping how developers manage terminal workspaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个用 Godot 构建前端界面、Rust 负责后端 PTY 多路复用的开源终端分屏工具，目标是提供比传统 tmux 更现代的界面和更流畅的用户体验，或改变开发者的终端工作空间管理方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b9c5839b13702d5f2622e22559baef7dde499463c7713592070c1a2062392348/godot-pty/gpty' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b9c5839b13702d5f2622e22559baef7dde499463c7713592070c1a2062392348/godot-pty/gpty' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49605433' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(302) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alvins82.github.io/hangar-harness-model-tests/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I tested 10 model/harness combinations on the same Three.js task</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我在同一 Three.js 任务上测试了 10 种模型/ harness 组合</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49605433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49605433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Benchmarking ten model/harness combinations on a Three.js task reveals how rendering performance and stability vary with toolchains. The results help developers select appropriate models and harnesses for real-time 3D in the browser, and highlight trade-offs between speed, memory, and accuracy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在同一 Three.js 任务上对十种模型与 harness 组合进行基准测试，揭示了不同工具链对渲染性能与稳定性的影响。结果为开发者在浏览器中实现实时3D时选择合适的模型与 harness 提供了参考，并揭示了速度、内存与精度之间的权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(303) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vincent.bernat.ch/en/blog/2026-spanning-tree' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An interactive tour of the spanning tree protocol</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An interactive tour of the spanning-tree protocol explains how bridges elect a root, assign port roles, and converge to a loop-free topology in layer-2 networks. The visuals help illuminate convergence dynamics and common pitfalls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对生成树协议的交互式讲解，阐释网桥如何选举根桥、分配端口角色，并收敛成无环的二层拓扑。可视化内容有助于理解收敛过程及常见误区。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49599609' data-tags='Programming,Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(311) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://queue.acm.org/doi/10.1145/3212477.3212479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>C Is Not a Low-Level Language (2018)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C 并非低级语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49599609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 82</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49599609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>C presents abstractions and undefined behaviors that distance it from true low-level programming; compilers and hardware details shape program semantics in subtle ways. Thinking of C as a high-level language with performance-minded features helps explain portability pitfalls, security bugs, and why reasoning about C often requires both language- and hardware-level knowledge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>C 带有抽象和未定义行为，这些特性使它不同于真正的底层编程语言；编译器和硬件细节会以微妙方式影响程序语义。把 C 看作带有性能导向特性的高级语言，有助于理解可移植性问题、安全漏洞以及为什么理解 C 需要兼顾语言和硬件层面的知识。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(312) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hugovergnes.github.io/little-lm-3-8b/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Training a 3.8B LLM to 0.384 CORE for $998</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demonstrates how to train a 3.8B parameter language model for under $1k using accessible hardware and data, achieving surprisingly strong results with efficient fine-tuning. The post outlines practical techniques like low-rank adaptation and careful data selection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示在不到一千美元的成本下，利用可获取的硬件与数据训练一个3.8B参数的语言模型，并通过高效微调获得出色表现。文中介绍了如低秩自适应、精挑数据等实用方法。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲坚持“简而精”的路径，专注于效率、韧性和差异化创新，在能源政策、科技治理或经济影响力方面的实际作用往往超出外界预期。文章指出审慎的政策选择、跨境协作和资金布局带来低调而显著的影响力，挑战对欧洲科技实力的偏见。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(321) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/smart-news/researchers-determine-how-to-spot-fake-ancient-pottery-using-the-earths-magnetic-field-180989441/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Researchers Spot Fake Ancient Pottery Using the Earth&#x27;s Magnetic Field</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究人员用地磁场识别伪造古陶器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580761' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580761' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers assess remanent magnetization in ancient pottery to distinguish authentic pieces from fakes. Leveraging Earth&#x27;s magnetic imprint preserved in clay, this approach adds a new tool for archaeologists confronting forgeries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员通过分析陶器中的剩磁来分辨真伪。利用黏土中保留的地磁记忆，这一方法为考古伪品鉴定增添了一种新工具。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(325) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gizmodo.com/aardman-is-selling-its-original-movie-puppets-this-month-2000807968' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Aardman (Wallace and Gromit) Is Selling Its Original Movie Puppets</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49592799' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49592799' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Aardman—famed for Wallace and Gromit—is selling its original movie puppets, offering fans a rare physical link to beloved films. The sale highlights how practical effects props can become collectible artifacts with cultural and monetary value.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Aardman 以《华莱士与格洛米特》而闻名，现在正在出售其原始电影用木偶，为粉丝提供与心爱的影片之间罕见的实体联系。此次拍卖突显实景特效道具如何成为具有文化和经济价值的收藏品。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(327) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thurrott.com/forums/microsoft/windows/thread/bing-wallpaper-showing-ad-for-harry-potter-and-fantastic-beasts-box-set' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bing Wallpaper showing Ad for Harry Potter and Fantastic beasts box set</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>必应桌面壁纸显示《哈利·波特》合集广告</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49599719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49599719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Users noticed the system wallpaper service displaying a commercial image for a Harry Potter box set, blurring the line between decorative background and advertising. The incident spotlights how default OS-provided wallpapers and curated image feeds can become ad vectors, raising UX and monetization questions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有用户发现系统桌面壁纸服务出现了《哈利·波特》合集的广告图片，这模糊了装饰性壁纸与广告之间的界限。这个现象凸显了默认操作系统壁纸和策展图像流可能被用作广告载体，带来用户体验和变现方面的讨论。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(328) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.edge.org/response-detail/27238' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Impedance Matching (2017)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阻抗匹配（2017）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49596274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49596274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An Edge essay argues for impedance matching across components to maximize information transfer and reduce bottlenecks in complex systems. The piece uses analogies to electrical engineering to emphasize design discipline.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇 Edge 阅读文献讨论跨组件的阻抗匹配，强调在复杂系统中通过对齐约束与接口以提升信息传递效率、降低瓶颈。通过电气工程类比指出设计应保持各环节的协调性与可预测性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.edge.org/sites/default/files/edge_logo.jpg' data-full='https://www.edge.org/sites/default/files/edge_logo.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(329) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>C*: Unifying Programming and Verification in C (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C*：在 C 语言中统一编程与验证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49612191' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49612191' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>C* proposes unifying programming and formal verification in C, enabling correctness proofs to accompany code and improve reliability in systems software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>C* 提出在 C 语言中将编程与形式验证统一起来，使正确性证明成为代码的一部分，提升系统软件的可靠性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AgentsDock provides an IDE tailored for building and testing agentic AI systems, offering tooling for evaluation, debugging, and experimentation with agents and environments. It lowers the friction of research, enabling rapid iteration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AgentsDock 是一个面向代理型 AI 研究的专用开发环境，集成评估、调试和实验工具，帮助研究者快速迭代、验证代理行为与环境交互。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>麦克白及其困境重新审视莎士比亚的悲剧，聚焦野心、罪愆和权力带来的腐蚀。文章挑战传统的英雄/反派划分，为领导力和叙事创作提供新的视角。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(334) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://uecker.codeberg.page/2026-09-05.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Implementation of GCC&#x27;s Nested Functions (vs. C++ Lambdas)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GCC 嵌套函數實現（與 C++ Lambda 的比較）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49575766' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 73</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49575766' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This post compares GCC&#x27;s nested functions (a C-like closure mechanism) with C++ lambdas, touching on syntax, capturing semantics, and portability concerns. It helps readers weigh when to prefer nested functions or lambdas based on project constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文比較 GCC 的嵌套函數（類 C 的閉包機制）與 C++ 的 Lambda，討論語法、捕獲語義與可移植性問題。讓讀者在專案條件下衡量何時應選用嵌套函數或 Lambda。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49615922' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(341) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.doubleword.ai/what-happens-when-a-gpu-writes-memory' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What happens when a GPU writes memory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPU 写内存时会发生什么</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49615922' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49615922' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GPU memory writes are subject to memory coherence, ordering, and asynchronous execution between CPU and GPU. When synchronization is mishandled, subtle bugs can arise in ML workloads or graphics pipelines, so understanding the hardware model helps improve performance and correctness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GPU 写内存时涉及与 CPU 的一致性、顺序和异步执行，若忽略同步会在 ML 或图形管线中暴露出难以察觉的 bug。理解硬件模型有助于提升吞吐量和正确性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/g1zo7y59/production/dcb77fb461abef734f055a2e26bc185969df7000-800x535.jpg?rect=0,58,800,420&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/g1zo7y59/production/dcb77fb461abef734f055a2e26bc185969df7000-800x535.jpg?rect=0,58,800,420&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
