<p class='hn-title'>(346) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.vidact.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vidact – a compiler that turns React into direct DOM operations</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vidact——将 React 转换为直接 DOM 操作的编译器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49542123' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49542123' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vidact compiles React components into direct DOM manipulation, bypassing the virtual DOM layer. This approach can reduce runtime overhead and open up new performance opportunities for React apps by emitting imperative updates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vidact 将 React 组件编译为直接的 DOM 操作，绕过虚拟 DOM。通过生成命令式的 DOM 更新，这种做法有望降低运行时开销，为 React 应用带来新的性能机会。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.vidact.dev/logo-64-dark.png' data-full='https://www.vidact.dev/logo-64-dark.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49678432' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(347) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.accuweather.com/en/hurricane/no-atlantic-hurricanes-by-sept-12-breaks-a-60-year-record/1932278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>No Atlantic hurricanes by Sept. 12 breaks a 60-year record</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>9月12日前大西洋无飓风，打破60年纪录</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49678432' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49678432' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An unusually quiet Atlantic hurricane season up to mid-September breaks a six-decade record, reflecting shifting climate patterns and the reliability of seasonal forecasts. The absence of storms reduces immediate risk but raises questions about anomalies and future activity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大西洋在9月12日前罕见无飓风，打破60年纪录，凸显气候模式的变化与预报的复杂性。虽降低短期风险，但也引发对极端天气趋势的关注。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49665864' data-tags='Show HN,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(348) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mirza-rizvi/ResolveHQ' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: ResolveHQ – A Helpdesk Built on Cloudflare Workers, D1, R2 and Queues</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：ResolveHQ——基于 Cloudflare Workers、D1、R2 与队列的帮助台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49665864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49665864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ResolveHQ shows a helpdesk built on Cloudflare’s edge stack (Workers, D1, R2) with a queuing system. It demonstrates an edge-first SaaS approach, highlighting fast responses and resilient operation by keeping most logic near users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ResolveHQ 展示了一个建立在 Cloudflare 边缘栈（Workers、D1、R2）和队列系统之上的帮助台。该示例体现了边缘优先的 SaaS 架构，强调近端处理带来的快速响应与更高的鲁棒性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3064675a41fc6d51bf5bfa427701b1160c8c5b13cc4d65609983bbc600adb64d/mirza-rizvi/ResolveHQ' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3064675a41fc6d51bf5bfa427701b1160c8c5b13cc4d65609983bbc600adb64d/mirza-rizvi/ResolveHQ' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(349) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/paradise-runner/toast' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Toast, a beautiful by default in terminal IDE</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：默认美观的终端 IDE</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Toast is a terminal-based IDE designed with sensible defaults to minimize setup friction and maximize focus. It emphasizes a clean, fast UX with editor features typical of GUI IDEs, but in a lightweight terminal form. The project demonstrates how developers are rethinking IDE ergonomics for terminal workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Toast 是一个以默认设置友好、追求高效聚焦的终端 IDE。它在终端形态中保留了常见的编辑器功能，强调简洁快速的用户体验，同时降低上手门槛。该项目展示了开发者如何在终端工作流中重新思考 IDE 的人体工学。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(350) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/09/05/technology/kenya-college-essays-ai.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kenyans Did College Students&#x27; Homework for Years. Then A.I. Arrived</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>肯尼亚人替大学生代写作业多年；AI 出现后</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49613642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49613642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Long-standing outsourcing of college assignments in Kenya dwindled as AI tools emerged, shifting workloads, incentives, and the high-touch tutoring market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>肯尼亚长期为大学生代写作业的现象在 AI 出现后发生变化，工作量、激励与高接触 tutoring 市场发生转变。文章探讨全球劳动与教育的深层影响。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(353) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/finance-and-economics/2026/09/04/the-jobs-apocalypse-is-postponed-an-ai-jobs-boom-is-here' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Initial effects of AI technology on employment look positive</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 技术对就业的初步影响呈现积极信号</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49596610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49596610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An Economist piece notes early indicators that AI adoption is not just displacing but also augmenting employment, with productivity gains creating new opportunities. It discusses how policy and training can sustain a positive job outlook.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该经济学家文章指出，人工智能初步影响显示积极信号，不仅在替代工作，还在提升生产力并创造新机会。文中还就政策与培训如何支撑积极的就业前景提出建议。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide to bring-up the Linux kernel on new hardware platforms, detailing steps like device tree bindings, drivers, and bootloaders, plus common pitfalls. It helps developers adapt the kernel to unfamiliar architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供在新硬件平台上启动 Linux 内核的实用指南，涵盖设备树绑定、驱动程序实现、引导加载等步骤，以及常见坑点，帮助开发者在陌生架构上完成适配。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TailTalk 是一个用 Rust 和 Tokio 构建的现代异步用户态 AppleTalk 协议栈，演示如何用高安全性语言实现旧有网络协议的现代化实现。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49591112' data-tags='AI,Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(356) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://direct.mit.edu/books/oa-monograph/6067/The-Microeconomics-of-Artificial-Intelligence' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Microeconomics of Artificial Intelligence (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人工智能的微观经济学（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49591112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49591112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open-access monograph analyzing how AI reshapes microeconomics: pricing of AI-enabled goods, productivity effects, firm strategy, data as capital, and competition with winner-take-all dynamics. It links theory to policy implications for businesses and regulators. It provides frameworks for evaluating AI adoption and its distributional impacts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一本开放获取的专著，系统分析人工智能如何重塑微观经济学：AI驱动商品定价、生产力与企业策略、数据资本的作用，以及可能的并购与市场集中。讨论了政策含义、竞争格局和企业在AI时代的投资取舍，为分析AI采用的经济影响提供框架。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49673392' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(357) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/994333/lg-responds-to-tv-spying-allegations' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LG responds to TV spying allegations</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LG回应电视监控指控</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49673392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49673392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LG publicly addresses privacy concerns tied to smart TVs, outlining what data is collected, how it’s used, and what options exist to limit telemetry. The episode underscores the ongoing tension between convenient smart features and user privacy in connected devices. It also highlights the importance of clear user controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LG就智能电视相关的隐私指控作出回应，解释收集的数据、用途以及限制遥测的选项。这一事件凸显了在连接设备中实现便利功能与保护用户隐私之间的博弈，并强调提供清晰的用户控制的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/01/LG-B4-Press-Image-3.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/01/LG-B4-Press-Image-3.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(358) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lucio.albenga.es/web-en/posts/2025/from-git-to-fossil.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>From Git to Fossil (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从 Git 到 Fossil</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49649864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49649864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comparative look at Fossil, a distributed VCS, and Git, discussing trade-offs in history, branching, and fossilized archives. It argues for preserving more durable, citable code histories.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对分布式版本控制系统 Fossil 与 Git 进行对比，探讨历史记录、分支和可持续存档的取舍，强调培养更耐久、可引用的代码历史的价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lucio.albenga.es/web-en/theme/images/Emacs-logo.png' data-full='https://lucio.albenga.es/web-en/theme/images/Emacs-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(359) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apnews.com/article/chile-el-nino-humboldt-penguins-vulnerable-injuries-rehabilitation-6e93b8d4eaef903e4c85d16ade7d5881' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Copper lined vest to help penguins with recovery</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>铜衬里背心帮助企鹅康复</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A veterinary rehabilitation effort using copper-lined vests to reduce injuries or infections in penguin rehabilitation, reflecting biomaterial applications in wildlife care.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(360) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/nefarius/vd_agent/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I refuse to let SPICE die</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不让SPICE死去</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49672641' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49672641' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SPICE—the long‑standing circuit simulator—remains active thanks to community efforts and forks that keep it compatible with modern tooling. Ongoing work blends older methodologies with newer workflows, preserving an essential tool for education and research in electronics. The piece is a tribute to sustaining open‑source engineering tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>依然活跃的SPICE电路仿真工具，得益于社区努力和分支保持与现代工具链的兼容。持续的工作在保留传统方法的同时融入新型工作流，为电子学教育和研究保留了一项不可或缺的开源工具。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49573629' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(362) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/security/2026/09/once-popular-for-attacking-ai-ascii-smuggling-is-embraced-by-spammers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft says email spammers are adopting ASCII smuggling</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软称邮件垃圾邮件发送者正在采用 ASCII 走私技术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49573629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49573629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft reports that email spammers are increasingly using ASCII smuggling to evade content filters, leveraging visually similar characters to slip past checks. This tactic, once seen in AI-targeted attacks, is re-emerging in more campaigns, prompting improved filtering and anomaly detection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软报告称邮件垃圾邮件发送者正日益使用 ASCII 走私来绕过内容过滤，利用视觉上相似的字符穿过检测。这一手法此前在针对 AI 的攻击中出现，如今在更多攻击中再次出现，促使加强过滤和异常检测。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/no-eyes-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/no-eyes-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49687869' data-tags='AI,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(363) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://singjupost.com/startalk-there-is-no-ai-really-its-just-people-w-jaron-lanier-transcript/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There Is No AI (It&#x27;s Just People) with Jaron Lanier</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>没有真正的人工智能——只是人类参与</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49687869' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49687869' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jaron Lanier argues there is no autonomous AI; current systems rely on human-generated data and labor. This view reframes AI as a social and economic artifact, highlighting data ownership, labor, and bias issues in machine learning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>贾伦·莱尼尔认为不存在真正的独立人工智能；现有系统本质上依赖人类数据与劳动。此观点将 AI 重新定位为社会与经济产物，凸显数据所有权、劳动及机器学习偏见等问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://singjupost.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/StarTalk-Podcast-There-Is-No-AI-Really-Its-Just-People-with-Jaron-Lanier.jpg' data-full='https://singjupost.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/StarTalk-Podcast-There-Is-No-AI-Really-Its-Just-People-with-Jaron-Lanier.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49679734' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49679734' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astronomers spotted a rogue black hole captured while accreting matter, offering a rare glimpse into the behavior of wandering black holes in the Milky Way and their feeding dynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>天文学家在天区观测到一只正在吞噬物质的流浪黑洞，为我们揭示银河系中漫游黑洞的吸积行为提供珍贵线索。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(365) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gwern.net/on-really-trying' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>On Really Trying (2009)</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>What does it mean to really try? The essay probes the psychology of effort, failure, and long-shot projects, challenging the assumption that motivation alone guarantees success. It offers pragmatic takeaways for anyone building ambitious, long-horizon work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨真正投入到艰难任务中的含义，分析努力、失败与回报之间的关系，以及在长期项目中动机和资源分配的现实考量。对从事长期、高强度工作的读者具有可操作的启示。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author reverse engineers the storage format of an undocumented database Cronos, enabling data extraction and migration without official docs. The post shows how to uncover data layout and encoding schemes to achieve interoperability. It illustrates the value and limits of reverse-engineering in data migration projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者对一款未公开文档的数据库 Cronos 的存储格式进行了逆向工程，使得在没有官方文档的情况下也能提取数据与迁移。文中展现了如何揭示数据布局与编码方案以实现互操作性，并讨论了逆向工程在数据迁移中的价值与局限。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical approach to safe web surfing, covering habits and tooling to reduce risk: use HTTPS, disable tracking, update software, and be mindful of phishing. It presents pragmatic steps rather than theoretical advice.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(369) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://leaflet.pub/p/did:plc:yhgc5rlqhoezrx6fbawajxlh/3muwrqenzfk2n' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GamersNexus and LG: Or why rooting your TV is a bad idea</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GamersNexus 与 LG：为什么 rooting 电视是个坏主意</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49605424' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49605424' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rooting a TV can unlock customization but introduces security and reliability risks. The piece with GamersNexus and LG argues that voiding warranties, bricking devices, or exposing attack surfaces outweigh potential hacks, urging users to stick to officially supported firmware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对智能电视进行 root 常会带来安全与稳定性风险，可能导致保修失效、设备变砖以及攻击面扩展。文章结合 GamersNexus 与 LG 的观点，强调官方固件的安全性与兼容性，劝告用户不要越狱。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(370) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.technologyreview.com/2026/08/18/1142188/ai-recursive-self-improvement/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI recursive self-improvement might not come so quickly after all</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 的递归自我改进或许不会迅速到来（2026年8月）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49687334' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49687334' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New analyses argue that rapid recursive self-improvement in AI is unlikely in the near term due to bottlenecks in alignment, data, and compute, suggesting progress will be incremental rather than transformative. The piece weighs hype against practical constraints shaping near-term AI advances.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新分析称，受制于对齐、数据和算力等瓶颈，AI 的递归自我改进在近期不太可能迅速实现，进展将更偏向增量。文章评估了炒作与现实约束对未来AI发展的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues for including Base84 in file-name conventions to improve cross-system robustness and metadata encoding, outlining use cases and potential trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张在文件名中使用 Base84 编码方案，以实现跨系统的鲁棒性与一致性，同时讨论潜在的权衡与实现要点。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 的 Sam Altman：在 2026 年上市将是不明智的</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49676849' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49676849' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sam Altman says going public in 2026 would be ill-advised, signaling OpenAI&#x27;s preference to stay private longer to focus on AI safety and long-term development. The stance hints at governance and capital strategy tensions for AI heavyweights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>萨姆·奥特曼表示在 2026 年上市将被视为不明智，表明 OpenAI 更愿意继续保持非上市状态以专注于 AI 安全与长期发展。此举揭示了 AI 巨头在治理与资本策略方面的博弈。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(385) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c5y75dl0jpko' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ancient cave art suggests first known humans in Ireland walked over from Wales</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>古代洞穴艺术揭示爱尔兰最早人类来自威尔士的证据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49600569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49600569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archaeologists interpret ancient cave art as evidence that the first known humans in Ireland may have migrated from Wales, challenging simplified models of post-glacial settlement. The finding adds color to cross-channel prehistoric connections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>考古学家解读洞穴艺术为爱尔兰最早人类可能来自威尔士的证据，这打破了简化的后冰期定居模型。此发现为跨海峡的史前联系增添了新线索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/7f1f/live/e10affc0-aaab-11f1-9eba-7de947fa7cd1.png' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/7f1f/live/e10affc0-aaab-11f1-9eba-7de947fa7cd1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(386) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.wsu.edu/press-release/2026/08/20/researchers-develop-electronic-skin-for-prosthetics-to-sense-temperature-and-pressure/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Electronic skin for prosthetics to sense temperature and pressure</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用于假肢的电子皮肤：感知温度与压力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49540090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49540090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers developed electronic skin for prosthetics that senses temperature and pressure, delivering richer tactile feedback. The advancement could improve dexterity and reduce phantom limb sensations, bringing prosthetic control closer to natural touch. Real-world adoption will hinge on durability and seamless nerve integration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员开发出用于假肢的电子皮肤，具备温度和压力传感能力，提供更丰富的触觉反馈。该进展有望提升假肢的灵活度并缓解幻肢痛，使控制更接近自然触感。实际落地将依赖耐用性与与神经系统的整合难易度。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(387) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://belderbos.dev/blog/how-libraries-run-rust-inside-python/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Libraries Run Rust Inside Python (With PyO3)</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49685037' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49685037' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PyO3 enables calling Rust libraries from Python, unlocking near-native performance where it matters. The note covers common usage patterns, performance considerations, and potential pitfalls when mixing the two ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PyO3 让 Rust 库能在 Python 中调用，从而在需要时获得接近原生的性能。文章讨论了常见的用法模式、性能考量以及在 Python 与 Rust 生态间引入依赖时的潜在坑点。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(388) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09153' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Procedural Graphs: Self-Evolving Execution Structures for LLM Agents</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Procedural Graphs propose self-evolving execution structures to guide LLM agents, enabling adaptive, task-aware planning. The approach aims to improve robustness and scalability of autonomous agents by letting their execution flows evolve with tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>过程图提出自进化执行结构来引导 LLM 代理，使其在任务中实现自适应、上下文感知的规划。通过让执行流程随任务演化，提升自治代理的鲁棒性和可扩展性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(394) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nber.org/papers/w35559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Replaceable but Employed: Automation and the Meaning of Work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>可替代却仍在使用：自动化与工作的意义</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49601814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49601814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An NBER paper argues that automation can replace routine tasks, yet humans remain employed due to complementary skills, organizational needs, and demand for problem-solving. It discusses policy implications for worker retraining and job quality as automation expands.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇 NBER 论文认为自动化可能取代常规任务，但人仍被雇用，因为需要互补技能、组织结构与解决问题的能力。讨论了在自动化扩张背景下对工人再培训与工作价值的政策影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nber.org/sites/default/files/2022-06/NBER-FB-Share-Tile-1200.jpg' data-full='https://www.nber.org/sites/default/files/2022-06/NBER-FB-Share-Tile-1200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(395) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/products/gemini-app/gemini-app-now-on-windows/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Gemini app is now available for Windows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini 应用现已登陆 Windows</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49653699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49653699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google&#x27;s Gemini app is now available on Windows, bringing desktop access to its AI features with system integration, notifications, and cross-device sync. This broadens reach for users who prefer native apps over browsers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google 的 Gemini 应用现已在 Windows 上线，提供桌面端的 AI 功能、系统集成、通知以及跨设备同步，方便偏好原生应用的用户使用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(396) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ersc.io/blog/what-comes-after-git' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What Comes After Git</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Git 之后会怎样</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49652985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49652985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Looks ahead at possible directions for version control beyond Git, including more atomic operations, improved history visualization, and collaboration primitives that rethink distributed workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展望超越 Git 的版本控制演进方向，可能包括更原子化的操作、改进的历史可视化，以及重新设计的协作原语，颠覆传统的分布式工作流。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(397) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jeffs.blog/p/defining-ai-psychosis-part-2-prolific' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Defining AI Psychosis. Part 2: &quot;Prolific AI Psychosis&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>定义 AI 精神病。第 2 部分：‘高产型 AI 精神病’</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49628880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49628880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece discusses a concept of AI psychosis, focusing on how prolific AI agents can exhibit unstable or overconfident behaviors, and how to mitigate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>此文讨论 AI 精神病的概念，聚焦于高产型 AI 代理可能展现的不稳定或过于自信的行为以及应对方法。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(398) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thelec.net/news/articleView.html?idxno=12835' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Samsung Debuts zHBM Prototype, Stacking Memory Directly on AI Accelerators</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>三星发布 zHBM 原型，将内存直接堆叠在 AI 加速器上</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49593896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49593896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Samsung unveils a zHBM prototype that stacks memory directly onto AI accelerators, aiming to boost bandwidth and cut latency for AI workloads. If scalable, this approach could reshape memory hierarchy in future AI chips and enable denser, faster inference hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>三星推出 zHBM 原型，直接将内存堆叠到 AI 加速器上，目标是提升带宽并降低 AI 工作负载的延迟。这一做法若规模化，可能重塑未来 AI 芯片的内存层级，使单位面积内获得更高密度和更快推理性能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.thelec.net/news/photo/202608/12835_12932_5527.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.thelec.net/news/photo/202608/12835_12932_5527.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49534396' data-tags='Energy,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(399) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chemisting.com/2026/09/02/why-are-there-no-flow-batteries-with-symmetric-ferrocyanide-electrolytes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why are there no flow batteries with symmetric ferrocyanide electrolytes?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么没有采用对称铁氰化物电解质的流动电池？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49534396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49534396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Symmetric ferrocyanide-based flow batteries are rare because redox potential windows, chemical stability, and membrane crossover create practical limits on efficiency and lifespan. Issues like pH-dependent decomposition, side reactions at cell potentials, and crossover-induced capacity fade make asymmetric or alternative chemistries more viable for commercial flow systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>采用对称铁氰化物电解质的流动电池很少见，主要是因为电位窗口、化学稳定性和隔膜渗透等因素在效率与寿命上带来实际限制。诸如受 pH 影响的分解、电池电位下的副反应以及渗透导致的容量衰减等问题，使得非对称或其它体系在商业化流动电池中更可行。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://chemisting.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/image.png' data-full='https://chemisting.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(400) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://justinjay.wang/methods-for-random-gradients/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Methods for Random Gradients (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2024 年的随机梯度方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49557986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49557986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A survey of methods for random gradients in 2024 surveys how injecting or leveraging gradient randomness can improve optimization, aiding exploration and preventing premature convergence. It discusses practical takeaways for training robust models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2024 年的随机梯度方法综述评估了在优化过程中引入或利用梯度随机性的策略，帮助提高探索性并避免过早收敛。面向机器学习从业者，给出可操作的实践要点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/e5/51/e5518248-64c6-46dd-941d-a7fd73d59c60/content/images/2024/12/gradients-cover-1.jpg' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/e5/51/e5518248-64c6-46dd-941d-a7fd73d59c60/content/images/2024/12/gradients-cover-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49626110' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(401) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chillphysicsenjoyer.substack.com/p/liesegang-rings' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Liesegang Rings</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>莱格根环</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49626110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49626110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores Liesegang rings, a striking pattern-forming phenomenon in chemical reactions, tracing history and explaining how diffusion and reaction fronts carve series of concentric bands. A look at how simple chemistry yields complex, emergent structures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 Liesegang 环现象——化学反应中的美丽催化条纹，追溯其历史并解释扩散与反应前沿如何形成同心环状带，揭示简单化学也能产生复杂的自组织结构。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(402) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://d2lang.com/blog/d2-non-profit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>D2 Is Non-Profit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>D2：非盈利项目</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49589768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49589768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>D2, a programming language, declares itself non-profit, shifting governance and development to a community-driven model. The move signals a focus on sustainability and openness over commercial aims. It invites contributors to shape the language&#x27;s trajectory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>D2 作为一种编程语言宣布自己为非盈利项目，将治理与开发带入社区驱动模式。此举强调可持续性与开放性优先于商业目标，期待社区贡献共同推动语言发展。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49594008' data-tags='Show HN,Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(405) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/timgordontg/engrim' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Engrim – A universal, local-first SQLite memory engine for AI CLIs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Engrim：用于 AI 命令行界面的本地优先 SQLite 内存引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49594008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49594008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Engrim is a universal, local-first SQLite memory engine for AI CLIs, enabling offline-friendly memory and state management for AI-powered CLIs. It promises consistency, portability, and data sovereignty for local AI workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Engrim 是一个通用的本地优先 SQLite 内存引擎，面向 AI 命令行界面。它为本地 AI 工作流提供离线记忆和状态管理，强调一致性、可移植性和数据主权。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/07e6c696606e410ce4221f47de3c8ae25b302951ecc9699b256ee0db12751608/timgordontg/engrim' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/07e6c696606e410ce4221f47de3c8ae25b302951ecc9699b256ee0db12751608/timgordontg/engrim' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(407) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC12965823/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Resistance Training Prescription for Muscle Function, Hypertrophy in Health</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>健康人群的肌功能与肌肥大阻力训练处方</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reviews evidence-based guidelines for resistance training to optimize muscle function and hypertrophy in healthy adults. It synthesizes dosage, intensity, progression, and safety considerations across different ages and fitness levels.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于证据的阻力训练指南，旨在提升健康人群的肌功能与肌肉肥大。综合了训练剂量、强度、进展与安全性要点，并就不同年龄与体能水平给出调整建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(408) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.remijouan.net/posts/libzmq-vsock-pyzmq/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Use Vsock with Libzmq</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Libzmq 中使用 Vsock</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49602615' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49602615' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shows how to use vsock as a transport layer with libzmq for inter-VM/container messaging. The guide includes practical configuration and Python examples, enabling lower-latency IPC across guest VMs or containers. This expands libzmq&#x27;s deployment scenarios beyond TCP.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了如何在 libzmq 中使用 vsock 作为跨虚拟机/容器的传输层实现消息通信，包括实际配置与 Python 示例，帮助实现更低延迟的进程间通讯。这扩大了 libzmq 的部署场景，超越了传统的 TCP。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(410) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cpak.it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cpak – OCI application package format for Linux desktops, servers and devices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cpak——面向 Linux 桌面、服务器及设备的 OCI 应用包格式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cpak proposes an OCI-compatible, cross-desktop/server/device packaging format to streamline Linux application distribution. It aims to reduce environmental fragmentation and improve install and security auditing workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cpak 提出一个与 OCI 兼容的跨桌面、跨服务器、跨设备的打包格式，旨在简化 Linux 应用分发并提升安装一致性与安全审计能力。它力求减少不同环境之间的碎片化。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(412) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aprilnea.me/en/blog/reverse-engineering-claude-code-antspace' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reverse-Engineering Claude Web&#x27;s MicroVM: Uncovering Anthropic&#x27;s Hidden Antspace</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>逆向工程 Claude Web 的 MicroVM：揭示 Anthropic 的隐藏 Antspace</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49653311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49653311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers reverse-engineer Claude Web&#x27;s MicroVM and uncover a hidden Antspace component, shedding light on how Anthropic isolates AI workloads. The findings raise questions about security, reproducibility, and the deployment of cloud-based AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员对 Claude Web 的 MicroVM 进行逆向分析，发现隐藏的 Antspace 组件，揭示 Anthropic 如何对 AI 工作负载进行隔离。结果引发关于安全性、可重复性及云端 AI 部署的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Neijuan (involution) describes intensified internal competition with diminishing returns in crowded systems, often used to critique China’s job market and broader high-pressure environments. It’s a lens for understanding modern work culture and social anxiety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>内卷指在资源有限且竞争激烈的环境中，内部竞争愈发激烈但收益递减。它常被用来批评中国的就业市场和更广泛的高压工作环境，是理解当代工作文化与社会焦虑的切口。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Show HN project outlines a self-hosted company OS that runs Claude Code and Codex agents per department, emphasizing data locality and governance. The setup spotlights how internal tooling can scale with privacy and control, though it requires maintenance.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(415) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.macrumors.com/2026/09/09/apple-announces-foldable-iphone-duo/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple Announces Foldable &#x27;iPhone Duo&#x27;</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49630666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49630666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49627646' data-tags='Show HN,Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(416) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Atomburstofficial/geiger' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Geiger – See every AI agent on your machine and what it can touch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Geiger——查看本机上的所有 AI 代理及其能接触的对象</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49627646' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49627646' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Geiger visualizes all AI agents running locally and maps their touchpoints, improving security, auditing, and control over AI in the workspace.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Geiger 可视化本机运行的所有 AI 代理及其可触及的对象，帮助提升安全性、审计与治理。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(417) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://codingpirate.com/understanding-computer-memory-architecture-ac9320110787' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Understanding Computer Memory Architecture and SSD Internals</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>理解计算机内存架构与SSD内部结构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49590199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49590199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This overview explains how data travels from CPU caches and DRAM to storage, and why SSD internals influence latency, endurance, and throughput. Grasping these details helps optimize performance and reliability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本概览讲解数据如何在CPU缓存、DRAM和存储之间流动，以及SSD内部结构如何影响延迟、耐久性与吞吐。掌握这些细节有助于从系统与应用层面优化性能与可靠性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vertumnus 提供可打印的农贸市场时令海报，数据驱动地显示应季蔬果。海报帮助家庭在采购和备餐时按时令规划，并且可离线使用，方便厨房使用。</p>

The piece shows how immersive storytelling can mobilize awareness and inspire action to protect fragile ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇作品通过数据叙事与地图展示濒临消失的林地，聚焦生物多样性损失、气候风险及保护机遇。通过沉浸式讲述唤起公众意识，推动保护脆弱生态系统的行动。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(426) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pages.cs.wisc.edu/~plonka/netgear-sntp/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flawed Routers Flood University of Wisconsin Internet Time Server (2003)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>有缺陷的路由器在2003年洪泛威斯康星大学的互联网时间服务器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49688391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49688391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2003 incident shows how flawed routing can flood critical time services, stressing the need for SNTP hardening and clean routing practices. The piece traces the misconfigurations and their impact on infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2003年的事件揭示了配置有误的路由器如何对关键的时间服务造成洪泛，强调 SNTP 服务器的防护和路由整洁的重要性。文中梳理了多处错误配置与对现代基础设施的教训。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入探讨生成 P3 平铺的算法——一种引人注目的几何图案，具有在图形、艺术与计算几何等领域的潜在应用。研究聚焦于构造性生成方法。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>灭绝的袋虎的咬合力与现存哺乳动物截然不同</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49549327' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49549327' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New paleontological work on the thylacine reveals bite mechanics that differ from living mammals, suggesting unusual jaw design and feeding strategies. These biomechanical insights shed light on its ecological role and evolution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对袋虎的新考古学研究显示其咬合力与现存哺乳动物不同，暗示其颚部结构与进食策略存在独特性。这些生物力学发现有助于理解其生态角色与进化路径。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>函數參數不是顏色。本文主張不要把函數參數與無關的標籤混為一談，呼籲建立更清晰的輸入與行為之間的心智模型。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>坏代码像葛藤一样蔓延</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49643059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49643059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bad code spreads through systems like Kudzu, creating creeping maintenance burdens and brittle architectures. The piece advocates disciplined coding, refactoring, and architectural hygiene to curb it before it becomes unmanageable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>坏代码像葛藤般蔓延，侵蚀系统并带来日益难以承受的维护成本与脆弱的架构。文章呼吁从编码规范、重构和架构卫生入手，防止问题蔓延。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(463) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://invezz.com/news/2026/09/11/nvidia-says-every-1-it-invests-brings-back-100-so-why-does-the-stock-keep-falling/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia dismisses &quot;circular financing&quot;, says every $1 it invests brings back $100</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英伟达否认“循环融资”，称每投资1美元可带来100美元回报</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49682319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49682319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nvidia defends its investment approach, claiming every dollar invested yields a hundred dollars in returns, framing capital allocation as a driver of ecosystem value. The claim comes as the stock drifts lower, highlighting tension between aggressive investments and market valuation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英伟达称每投资1美元就带来100美元回报，回应对其循环融资策略的质疑。股价持续走低之际，这一表态凸显其对生态系统投资的信心与市场对估值的担忧之间的拉扯。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://invezz-wp-media.lon1.digitaloceanspaces.com/2026/07/image-4-1783511691.png' data-full='https://invezz-wp-media.lon1.digitaloceanspaces.com/2026/07/image-4-1783511691.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(471) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://calif.io/research/weworm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WeWorm: Zero-Click WeChat Worm</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WeWorm：零点击微信蠕虫</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49669276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49669276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes WeWorm, a zero-click worm that can propagate through WeChat without user interaction by exploiting platform weaknesses. It highlights the security implications for messaging ecosystems and recommends defense-in-depth measures like patching, sandboxing, and protocol hardening.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 WeWorm——一种无需用户交互即可在微信生态内传播的蠕虫，利用平台漏洞实现扩散。强调对消息生态系统的安全冲击，并提出分层防护，如及时修补、沙箱化和协议层加强等对策。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(472) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://codon.org.uk/~mjg59/blog/p/systemio-conflicts-are-not-firmware-bugs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SystemIO conflicts are not firmware bugs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SystemIO冲突并非固件错误</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49642396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49642396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that System.IO conflicts are not firmware bugs but issues rooted in software layers or OS scheduling, calling for better debugging methodologies and architectural fixes. It emphasizes proper attribution to software design rather than blaming hardware firmware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中认为System.IO冲突并非固件错误，而是软件层或操作系统调度等因素所致，需改进调试方法并从架构层面着手修复。强调应把问题归因于软件设计，而非硬件固件。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Summarizes a detailed roadmap for evaluating solar geoengineering deployments, outlining key research gaps, governance challenges, and risk-management trade-offs. It frames the debate around ethics, transparency, and incremental testing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>概述了评估太阳地球工程部署的详细路线图，指出关键研究差距、治理挑战与风险管理的取舍。将讨论框架置于伦理、透明度以及渐进式测试之上。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49673281' data-tags='Culture,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(475) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.npr.org/2026/09/08/nx-s1-5955618/is-it-time-for-a-luddite-renaissance' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is it time for a Luddite Renaissance?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>是时候来一场路德派复兴吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49673281' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49673281' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The NPR piece questions whether society should slow or rethink rapid tech adoption, weighing productivity gains against social costs and ethical concerns. It advocates thoughtful pacing, public dialogue, and safeguards to ensure technology serves broad human interests.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章质疑是否应放慢或重新审视对新技术的快速采用，权衡生产力收益与社会成本和伦理问题。呼吁进行更审慎的节奏控制、公众对话与多方保障，确保科技惠及更多人。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(477) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://davidbombal.com/your-compiler-can-undo-your-security-checks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Compiler Can Undo Your Security Checks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>编译器可能撤销你的安全检查</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49672474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49672474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Compilers can undermine security checks through optimization, reordering, or code-generation tricks, potentially bypassing runtime protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>编译器可能通过优化、重新排序或代码生成等手段，撤销或绕过运行时的安全检查。文章给出具体示例，并倡导在设计中采用分层防护与可验证的检查，以抵御编译器层面的规避。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://davidbombal.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/your-compiler-can-undo-your-security-checks.jpg' data-full='https://davidbombal.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/your-compiler-can-undo-your-security-checks.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(478) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://community.signalusers.org/t/registration-without-a-phone-number/2222?page=10' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Registration without a phone number on Signal will use zero-knowledge proofs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Signal 将在注册阶段无需手机号码，采用零知识证明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49689048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 18</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49689048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Signal is testing a phone-number-free registration using zero-knowledge proofs, letting users create accounts without sharing a phone number. This reduces SIM swap risk and privatizes identity, but introduces onboarding and trust challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Signal 正在测试无需手机号码的注册方式，利用零知识证明来实现去绑定身份。此举能提升隐私、降低 SIM 卡冒充风险，但在落地时需解决新用户引导与信任问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://community.signalusers.org/uploads/db3609/original/3X/2/a/2aae74b9e7c4af45dfcd09cfa6e9c90fb3b7534c.png' data-full='https://community.signalusers.org/uploads/db3609/original/3X/2/a/2aae74b9e7c4af45dfcd09cfa6e9c90fb3b7534c.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(479) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/legal/litigation/revolut-confirms-sensitive-customer-data-breach-falling-fake-government-requests-2026-09-12/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Revolut confirms customer data breach, falling for fake government requests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Revolut 确认客户数据泄露，因伪政府请求而受影响</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49674666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 18</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49674666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Revolut disclosed that customer data was breached after staff followed fake government requests, highlighting social engineering as a vector. The incident underscores the need for stronger verification and data-handling controls in fintech support workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Revolut 透露，部分客户数据因员工在假政府请求下外泄，凸显社交工程成为攻击向量。事件强调在金融科技客服流程中需要更强的身份与请求验证，以及更严格的数据处理控制。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(486) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.autodidacts.io/ode-to-metadata/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ode to Metadata</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>元数据颂歌</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49644241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 14</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49644241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Metadata underpins search, privacy, and system design; the piece reflects on why metadata matters more than we realize and how it shapes software and security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>元数据在搜索、隐私和系统设计中的作用被强调，文章反思我们常忽视的元数据的重要性及其潜在影响。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49626140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 14</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49626140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Yale Review piece delves into Moby-Dick as a meditation on the blank page, connecting narrative form with creative cognition when facing indeterminacy. It revisits how great literature negotiates possibility and meaning in the presence of a white space.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文以《白鲸》为例，探讨面对空白页时的吸引力与恐惧，将叙事形式与创作认知联系起来，揭示不确定性中潜在的可能性与意义如何在文本中显现。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍20世纪哲学家贡特·安德斯，他对大众媒介与技术发展带来灾难性的前景提出警示，将其对现代技术社会的批判与当下的自动化与存在风险议题联系起来。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(493) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Dillonsmart/docket' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Docket – Per-commit evidence records for agent-written code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Docket——为代理编写代码提供逐次提交证据记录</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49685642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 10</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49685642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Docket proposes per-commit evidence records to trace decisions made by code produced by autonomous agents, enabling provenance, accountability, and reproducibility. The project aims to make AI-assisted coding auditable by storing verifiable records alongside commits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Docket 提议为由自主代理生成的代码提供逐次提交证据记录，以追踪决策来源、提升可追溯性和可重复性。该项目通过在提交中附加可验证记录，使 AI 辅助编码具备可审计性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(495) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.katjahoyer.uk/p/the-gdr-and-vietnam-from-fake-coffee' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The GDR and Vietnam: From Fake Coffee to Coffee Empire</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德意志民主共和国与越南：从假咖啡到咖啡帝国</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49649164' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 9</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49649164' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece traces how East Germany manipulated coffee trade with Vietnam, including fake shipments to meet quotas and extend influence, shaping Vietnam’s coffee sector amid Cold War dynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章梳理东德在越南贸易中的“假咖啡”操控，借此达到配额与影响力扩张的目的，最终影响越南的咖啡产业，揭示商品政治与冷战格局的交织。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(496) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cacm.acm.org/blogcacm/why-is-privacy-so-hard/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why is privacy so hard?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>隐私为何如此难？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49688766' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 8</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49688766' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Privacy is hard due to tangled data ecosystems, network effects, and misaligned incentives across platforms. The piece argues for privacy-by-design, pragmatic regulation, and user-centric approaches to make privacy workable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>隐私之所以难，是因为数据生态、网络效应与平台激励错位叠加。文章主张在设计、监管与用户为中心的取向上协同，才能让隐私在现实世界中变得可行。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49675734' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(497) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/levelsio/status/2098839424356270163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>With regulatory capture we will speedrun to a new feudalist time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>有监管俘获，我们将进入新的封建时代</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49675734' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 8</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49675734' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that regulatory capture by industry incumbents accelerates a feudal-like tech governance regime, stifling innovation and centralizing power. It warns policymakers to guard against capture to sustain open, competitive ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为行业既得利益对监管的影响将推动出现类似封建时代的治理格局，抑制创新并集中权力。警示政策制定者防范监管被俘获，以维持开放、竞争的生态系统。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49647860' data-tags='Design,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，当局在外交官住所发现儿童虐待影像后将其秘密遣返，引发对豁免权和审查程序的质疑。此事凸显了外交特权与调查监督之间的张力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(500) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/GrapheneOS/status/2098792171058889006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GrapheneOS: Nearly all of our recent posts on Hacker News are being flagged</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49673372' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 6</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49673372' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS notes that nearly all its recent Hacker News posts have been flagged, raising questions about moderation, signal quality, and platform biases affecting dev communities. It underscores the friction between security-focused projects and community discussions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS 指出其在 Hacker News 的最近大部分帖子都被标记，引发关于内容审核、信息质量以及平台偏向对开发者社区的影响等问题的讨论，凸显安全导向项目与社区讨论之间的摩擦。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(501) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://untranslatedjp.substack.com/p/japans-book-scene-is-quietly-moving' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Japan&#x27;s book scene is quietly moving from bookstores to libraries</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本的书业正在悄然从书店转向图书馆</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49677916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 3</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49677916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes a shift in Japan towards libraries as primary access points for books, reflecting changes in lending models and digital access. The trend could influence publishing strategies and reader discovery in a data-driven, AI-influenced landscape.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>描述日本在把图书馆作为主要获取书籍入口的趋势，反映出借阅模式与数字访问的变化。此趋势可能影响出版策略，并在数据驱动、AI 影响的环境中改变读者发现书籍的方式。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(502) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tokendelivery.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Determinstic LLM inference for lowest price Gemma 4, with Windows XP</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49674280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 3</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49674280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

It discusses tradeoffs between determinism, performance, and platform limitations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示了在 Windows XP 上以极低成本实现 Gemma 4 的确定性推理，说明对老旧硬件也能在精心工程下运行现代模型。文中探讨确定性、性能与平台限制之间的权衡。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(503) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Fenish/decky' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Decky: Open-source DIY stream deck</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Decky：开源DIY流媒体控制板</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49670560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 3</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49670560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces Decky, an open-source, DIY alternative to commercial stream decks with a plugin ecosystem and cross-platform support. It enables custom macro keys for streaming, productivity, and gaming, driven by community contributions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 Decky，这是一个开源的 DIY 流媒体控制板替代方案，具备插件生态和跨平台支持。它为流媒体、生产力和游戏等场景提供自定义快捷键，并由社区贡献推动发展。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49637544' data-tags='Science,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(504) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.d3s.mff.cuni.cz/files/publications/antoch_experimental_2026.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Experimental Evaluation Methodology for the Era of No Steady Performance [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无稳定性能时代的实验评估方法论</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49637544' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 3</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49637544' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper proposes an evaluation framework for systems under fluctuating performance, covering metrics and repeatability for no-stable-performance environments. It argues for new methodologies to reflect real-world variability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文提出在性能不稳定的时代进行系统评估的实验框架，涵盖指标与可重复性等要点，面向真实工作负载中的波动场景。强调采用新的方法论以真实反映实际性能变动。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
